### ARCHIVO: lib\features\auth\qr_scanner_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:firebase_messaging/firebase_messaging.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:mobile_scanner/mobile_scanner.dart';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:device_info_plus/device_info_plus.dart';
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import '../../core/services/cache_service.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../core/services/session_events.dart';
import '../../core/repositories/user_repository.dart';
import '../../core/router/app_router.dart';

class QrScannerScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const QrScannerScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<QrScannerScreen> createState() => _QrScannerScreenState();
}

class _QrScannerScreenState extends ConsumerState<QrScannerScreen> {
  final CacheService _cacheService = CacheService();
  bool _isProcessing = false;
  MobileScannerController cameraController = MobileScannerController();

  void _onQRDetected(BarcodeCapture capture) {
    if (_isProcessing) return;

    final barcodes = capture.barcodes;
    if (barcodes.isEmpty) return;

    final code = barcodes.first.rawValue;
    if (code == null || code.isEmpty) return;

    setState(() => _isProcessing = true);

    _processQRData(code);
  }

    Future<void> _processQRData(String rawData) async {
    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse(rawData);

      String? token;
      String? server;

      if (uri.scheme == 'gijac') {
        token = uri.queryParameters['token'];
        server = uri.queryParameters['server'];
      } else if (uri.scheme == 'http' || uri.scheme == 'https') {
        token = uri.queryParameters['token'];
        server = uri.queryParameters['server'];
      } else {
        try {
          final json = jsonDecode(rawData);
          token = json['token'];
          server = json['server'];
        } catch (_) {}
      }

      if (token != null && server != null) {
        // 1. Guardar servidor temporalmente
        await _cacheService.saveServerUrl(server);
         await _cacheService.saveServerUrl(server);

        // 2. Liberar la cámara: ya se leyó el código (evita que siga generando cuadros mientras se registra)
        try {
          await cameraController.stop();
        } catch (_) {}

        // 3. Obtener info del celular
        final deviceInfoPlugin = DeviceInfoPlugin();
        Map<String, dynamic> deviceData = {
          'nombre_dispositivo': 'Desconocido',
          'modelo': null,
          'sistema_operativo': 'Desconocido',
          'version_so': null,
          'fcm_token': null,
        };

        bool registrado = false;
        try {
          if (Platform.isAndroid) {
            final android = await deviceInfoPlugin.androidInfo;
            deviceData = {
              'token': token,
              'nombre_dispositivo': android.model ?? 'Android',
              'modelo': android.model,
              'sistema_operativo': 'Android',
              'version_so': android.version.release,
              'fcm_token': null,
              'ip': null,
            };
          } else if (Platform.isIOS) {
            final ios = await deviceInfoPlugin.iosInfo;
            deviceData = {
              'token': token,
              'nombre_dispositivo': ios.name ?? 'iPhone',
              'modelo': ios.model,
              'sistema_operativo': 'iOS',
              'version_so': ios.systemVersion,
              'fcm_token': null,
              'ip': null,
            };
          }

          try {
            deviceData['fcm_token'] = await FirebaseMessaging.instance.getToken();
          } catch (e) {
            debugPrint('⚠️ No se pudo obtener el token FCM: $e');
          }

          // 4. Enviar a Laravel (reintenta: justo después de instalar, la red puede tardar en estabilizarse)
          for (var i = 0; i < 3 && !registrado; i++) {
            registrado = await ApiService().registerDevice(deviceData);
            if (!registrado && i < 2) await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));
          }
        } catch (e) {
          debugPrint('⚠️ Error al registrar dispositivo: $e');
        }

        if (!registrado) {
          _showError('No se pudo vincular el dispositivo. Revisa tu conexión e inténtalo de nuevo.');
          try {
            await cameraController.start();
          } catch (_) {}
          return;
        }

        // 5. Guardar el token
        await _cacheService.saveDeviceToken(token);

        // Guardar el perfil en caché (ya con sesión); si falla, el panel lo cargará después
        try {
          await UserRepository(ApiService(), _cacheService).getProfile().timeout(const Duration(seconds: 10));
        } catch (e) {
          debugPrint('⚠️ Perfil no cargado tras escanear: $e');
        }

        // Con la sesión ya guardada: pide el permiso de notificaciones y conecta el tiempo real sin reiniciar la app
        SessionEvents.onLogin?.call();

        if (mounted) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text('✅ Dispositivo vinculado exitosamente'),
              backgroundColor: Color(0xFF25D366),
              behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            ),
          );

          await Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
          appRouter.go('/dashboard');
        }
      } else {
        _showError('Código QR no válido');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _showError('Error al procesar el QR');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
      }
    }
  }

  /// Alternativa a la cámara: elegir de la galería una imagen (captura o foto) que tenga el QR
  Future<void> _desdeGaleria() async {
    if (_isProcessing) return;
    try {
      final img = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: ImageSource.gallery);
      if (img == null) return;

      setState(() => _isProcessing = true);
      final captura = await cameraController.analyzeImage(
        img.path,
        formats: const [BarcodeFormat.qrCode],
      );
      final codigo = (captura == null || captura.barcodes.isEmpty) ? null : captura.barcodes.first.rawValue;

      if (codigo == null || codigo.isEmpty) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
        _showError('No se encontró un código QR en la imagen');
        return;
      }
      await _processQRData(codigo); // al terminar libera _isProcessing
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('⚠️ No se pudo leer el QR de la imagen: $e');
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isProcessing = false);
      _showError('No se pudo leer la imagen');
    }
  }

  void _showError(String message) {
    if (mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text(message),
          backgroundColor: Colors.red,
          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    cameraController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.black,
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          MobileScanner(
            controller: cameraController,
            onDetect: _onQRDetected,
          ),

          Center(
            child: Container(
              width: 250,
              height: 250,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: const Color(0xFF25D366),
                  width: 3,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          if (_isProcessing)
            Container(
              color: Colors.black54,
              child: const Center(
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Color(0xFF25D366)),
              ),
            ),

          Positioned(
            top: 50,
            left: 20,
            child: IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white, size: 28),
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            ),
          ),

          Positioned(
            bottom: 40,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            child: Center(
              child: OutlinedButton.icon(
                onPressed: _desdeGaleria,
                icon: const Icon(Icons.image_outlined, color: Colors.white),
                label: const Text('Elegir imagen del QR', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.white54)),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          Positioned(
            bottom: 100,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            child: const Text(
              'Apunta la cámara al código QR',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\auth\splash_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/router/app_router.dart';
import '../../core/services/cache_service.dart';

class SplashScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SplashScreen> createState() => _SplashScreenState();
}

class _SplashScreenState extends State<SplashScreen> {
  final CacheService _cacheService = CacheService();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _checkSession();
  }

  Future<void> _checkSession() async {
    // Esperar un momento para mostrar el splash
    await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2));

    // Verificar si hay datos guardados
    final hasData = await _cacheService.hasDeviceData();

    if (!mounted) return;

    if (hasData) {
      // Ya hay token y servidor, ir al dashboard
      appRouter.go(Routes.dashboard);
    } else {
      // No hay datos, ir a escanear QR
      appRouter.go(Routes.qrLogin);
    }
  }

    @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF075E54), // Fondo verde oscuro
      body: Center(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            // LOGO CON FONDO VERDE
            Container(
              width: 120,
              height: 120,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFF25D366), // Fondo verde claro
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(28),
                boxShadow: [
                  BoxShadow(
                    color: const Color(0xFF25D366).withOpacity(0.4),
                    blurRadius: 30,
                    offset: const Offset(0, 10),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20), // Espacio interno para el logo
              child: Image.asset(
                'assets/images/logo_gmb_blanco.png',
                fit: BoxFit.contain,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            const Text(
              'GIJAC',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Colors.white,
                fontSize: 28,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                letterSpacing: 2,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text(
              'MESSAGE BUSINESS',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: Color(0xFF25D366),
                fontSize: 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                letterSpacing: 4,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 48),
            const SizedBox(
              width: 24,
              height: 24,
              child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                color: Color(0xFF25D366),
                strokeWidth: 2,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
class ChatsListScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ChatsListScreen({super.key});

  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final chats = ref.watch(filteredChatsProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(

      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton(

            child: TextField(
              onChanged: (v) =>
                  ref.read(chatSearchProvider.notifier).state = v,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                hintText: 'Buscar conversaciones',
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(
            height: 44,
            child: ListView(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
              children: [
                _chip(ref, ChatFilter.all, 'Todos', filter),
                _chip(ref, ChatFilter.unread, 'No leídos', filter),
                _chip(ref, ChatFilter.bot, 'Chatbot', filter),
                _chip(ref, ChatFilter.resolved, 'Resueltos', filter),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Expanded(
            child: chats.isEmpty
                ? const Center(child: Text('No hay conversaciones'))
                : RefreshIndicator(
                    onRefresh: () => ref.read(chatsProvider.notifier).loadChats(),
                    child: ListView.separated(
                    physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                    itemCount: chats.length,
                    separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const Divider(
                        height: 1, indent: 84),
                    itemBuilder: (_, i) =>
                        _ChatTile(chat: chats[i]),
                  )),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _newChat(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (_) => const _ContactPicker(),
    );
  }

  Widget _chip(
      WidgetRef ref, ChatFilter value, String label, ChatFilter current) {
    final selected = value == current;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
      child: ChoiceChip(
        label: Text(label),
        selected: selected,
        onSelected: (_) => ref.read(chatFilterProvider.notifier).state = value,
        showCheckmark: false,
        selectedColor: AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
        labelStyle: TextStyle(
          color: selected ? AppColors.secondary : null,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ChatTile extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _ChatTile({required this.chat});
  final Chat chat;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return ListTile(
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 6),
      onTap: () {
        ref.read(chatsProvider.notifier).markRead(chat.id);
        context.push(
          '/chats/${chat.id}',
          extra: {
            'nombre': chat.contact.name,
          },
        );
      },
      leading: AppAvatar(
        initials: chat.contact.initials,
        colorValue: chat.contact.avatarColorValue,
        isOnline: chat.contact.isOnline,
        radius: 26,
      ),
      title: Row(
        children: [
          if (chat.isPinned)
            const Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(right: 4),
              child: Icon(Icons.push_pin, size: 14, color: AppColors.secondary),
            ),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(chat.contact.name,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
          ),
          if (chat.hasActivity)
          Text(
            TimeFormat.relative(chat.lastTime),
            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: chat.unreadCount > 0
                  ? AppColors.primary
                  : theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
              fontWeight:
                  chat.unreadCount > 0 ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w400,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      subtitle: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            _statusIcon(),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                chat.lastMessage,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.65),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            if (chat.isMuted)
              Icon(Icons.volume_off,
                  size: 16,
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
            if (chat.unreadCount > 0)
              Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 6),
                padding:
                    const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                constraints:
                    const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 22, minHeight: 22),
                child: Text('${chat.unreadCount}',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _statusIcon() {
    final (label, color) = switch (chat.status) {
      ChatStatus.bot => ('Bot', AppColors.botAccent),
      ChatStatus.pending => ('Pend.', AppColors.warning),
      ChatStatus.resolved => ('OK', AppColors.success),
      ChatStatus.open => ('', AppColors.primary),
    };
    if (label.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
      child: StatusBadge(label: label, color: color),
    );
  }
}


class _ContactPicker extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const _ContactPicker();

  @override
  ConsumerState<_ContactPicker> createState() => _ContactPickerState();
}

class _ContactPickerState extends ConsumerState<_ContactPicker> {
  String _q = '';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final all = ref.watch(chatsProvider);
    final q = _q.toLowerCase();
    final list = all
        .where((c) =>
            q.isEmpty ||
            c.contact.name.toLowerCase().contains(q) ||
            c.contact.phone.contains(q))
        .toList();
    final height = MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.75;
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
      child: SizedBox(
        height: height,
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 8),
              child: TextField(
                autofocus: true,
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _q = v),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  hintText: 'Buscar contacto o teléfono',
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: list.isEmpty
                  ? const Center(child: Text('Sin resultados'))
                  : ListView.builder(
                      itemCount: list.length,
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                        final c = list[i];
                        return ListTile(
                          leading: AppAvatar(
                            initials: c.contact.initials,
                            colorValue: c.contact.avatarColorValue,
                            radius: 22,
                          ),
                          title: Text(c.contact.name,
                              maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
                          subtitle: Text(c.contact.phone),
                          onTap: () {
                            Navigator.of(context).pop();
                            context.push('/chats/${c.id}',
                                extra: {'nombre': c.contact.name});
                          },
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\chat_search_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/message.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../core/utils/time_format.dart';

/// Buscar texto dentro de la conversación (en todo el historial, no solo lo cargado).
class ChatSearchScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChatSearchScreen({super.key, required this.chatId});
  final String chatId;

  @override
  State<ChatSearchScreen> createState() => _ChatSearchScreenState();
}

class _ChatSearchScreenState extends State<ChatSearchScreen> {
  final _api = ApiService();
  final _controller = TextEditingController();
  Timer? _debounce;
  List<Message> _resultados = [];
  bool _buscando = false;
  String _consulta = '';
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _cambio(String texto) {
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 450), () => _buscar(texto.trim()));
  }

  Future<void> _buscar(String texto) async {
    if (texto.length < 2) {
      setState(() {
        _resultados = [];
        _consulta = '';
        _error = null;
      });
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _buscando = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final r = await _api.buscarMensajes(widget.chatId, texto);
      if (!mounted || _controller.text.trim() != texto) return;
      setState(() {
        _resultados = r;
        _consulta = texto;
        _buscando = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _buscando = false;
          _error = 'No se pudo buscar. Revisa tu conexión.';
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: TextField(
          controller: _controller,
          autofocus: true,
          onChanged: _cambio,
          textInputAction: TextInputAction.search,
          onSubmitted: (t) => _buscar(t.trim()),
          decoration: const InputDecoration(
            hintText: 'Buscar en esta conversación',
            border: InputBorder.none,
            enabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
            focusedBorder: InputBorder.none,
            filled: false,
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          if (_controller.text.isNotEmpty)
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.close),
              onPressed: () {
                _controller.clear();
                _buscar('');
              },
            ),
        ],
        bottom: _buscando
            ? const PreferredSize(preferredSize: Size.fromHeight(3), child: LinearProgressIndicator(minHeight: 3))
            : null,
      ),
      body: _error != null
          ? Center(child: Text(_error!))
          : _consulta.isEmpty
              ? const Center(child: Text('Escribe al menos 2 letras'))
              : _resultados.isEmpty && !_buscando
                  ? Center(child: Text('Sin resultados para "$_consulta"'))
                  : ListView.separated(
                      itemCount: _resultados.length,
                      separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const Divider(height: 1),
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                        final m = _resultados[i];
                        return ListTile(
                          title: _resaltado(m.text ?? '', _consulta, theme),
                          subtitle: Text(
                            '${m.isOutgoing ? 'Tú' : 'Cliente'} · ${TimeFormat.dayLabel(m.time)} ${TimeFormat.clock(m.time)}',
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
    );
  }

  /// Texto con la coincidencia resaltada
  Widget _resaltado(String texto, String q, ThemeData theme) {
    final i = texto.toLowerCase().indexOf(q.toLowerCase());
    if (i < 0) return Text(texto, maxLines: 3, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis);
    return Text.rich(
      TextSpan(children: [
        TextSpan(text: texto.substring(0, i)),
        TextSpan(
          text: texto.substring(i, i + q.length),
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: theme.colorScheme.primary),
        ),
        TextSpan(text: texto.substring(i + q.length)),
      ]),
      maxLines: 3,
      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
    );
  }
}

```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\conversation_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:record/record.dart';

import '../../core/models/message.dart';
import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../contacts/media_gallery_screen.dart';
import 'chat_search_screen.dart';
import '../../core/utils/time_format.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import 'widgets/message_bubble.dart';

class ConversationScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ConversationScreen({super.key, required this.chatId, this.nombre});
  final String chatId;
  final String? nombre;

  @override
  ConsumerState<ConversationScreen> createState() => _ConversationScreenState();
}

class _ConversationScreenState extends ConsumerState<ConversationScreen> {
  final _controller = TextEditingController();
  final _scrollController = ScrollController();
  bool _mostrarBajar = false; // botón para volver al último mensaje

  // Refresco automático (estados y mensajes nuevos) mientras el chat está abierto
  late final ConversationNotifier _conversacion;

  // Grabación de audio
  final AudioRecorder _grabadora = AudioRecorder();
  bool _grabando = false;
  int _segundos = 0;
  Timer? _grabacionTimer;
  static const _maxAudioSeg = 5 * 60;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _conversacion = ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier);

    // AGREGADO: Listener para paginación (cargar mensajes antiguos al hacer scroll arriba)
    _scrollController.addListener(_onScroll);

    // AGREGADO: Cargar mensajes de la API al entrar
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).loadMessages(widget.chatId);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _conversacion.leaveChat(widget.chatId);
    _grabacionTimer?.cancel();
    _grabadora.dispose();
    _controller.dispose();
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // AGREGADO: Lógica de scroll para cargar historia
  // La lista está invertida (reverse): offset 0 = último mensaje, y el final del scroll = los más antiguos.
  void _onScroll() {
    if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
    final pos = _scrollController.position;
    if (pos.pixels >= pos.maxScrollExtent - 300) {
      ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).loadOlderMessages(widget.chatId);
    }
    final lejos = pos.pixels > 400;
    if (lejos != _mostrarBajar) setState(() => _mostrarBajar = lejos);
  }

  // MODIFICADO: Ahora llama a la API real
  bool get _puedeEnviar => ref.read(permisoEnvioProvider);

  void _send() {
    if (!_puedeEnviar) return;
    final text = _controller.text;
    if (text.trim().isEmpty) return;

    _controller.clear();

    ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).sendMessage(widget.chatId, text);
    _scrollToBottom();
  }

  void _scrollToBottom() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
        _scrollController.animateTo(
          0,
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          curve: Curves.easeOut,
        );
      }
    });
  }

  bool get _nearBottom => !_scrollController.hasClients || _scrollController.offset < 200;

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------- audio

  Future<void> _iniciarGrabacion() async {
    if (!_puedeEnviar) return;
    try {
      if (!await _grabadora.hasPermission()) {
        _aviso('Da permiso al micrófono para grabar audios.');
        return;
      }
      final dir = await getTemporaryDirectory();
      final ruta = '${dir.path}/audio_${DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch}.m4a';
      await _grabadora.start(
        const RecordConfig(encoder: AudioEncoder.aacLc, bitRate: 64000, sampleRate: 44100),
        path: ruta,
      );
      setState(() {
        _grabando = true;
        _segundos = 0;
      });
      _grabacionTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) {
        if (!mounted) return;
        setState(() => _segundos++);
        if (_segundos >= _maxAudioSeg) _terminarGrabacion(enviar: true);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      _aviso('No se pudo iniciar la grabación.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _terminarGrabacion({required bool enviar}) async {
    if (enviar && !_puedeEnviar) enviar = false; // la ventana de 24 h se cerró: se descarta el audio
    _grabacionTimer?.cancel();
    final duracion = _segundos;
    String? ruta;
    try {
      ruta = await _grabadora.stop();
    } catch (_) {}
    if (mounted) setState(() => _grabando = false);
    if (ruta == null) return;

    final archivo = File(ruta);
    if (!enviar || duracion < 1) {
      if (await archivo.exists()) await archivo.delete();
      return;
    }
    ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).sendMedia(
          widget.chatId,
          archivo,
          MessageType.audio,
          fileName: archivo.uri.pathSegments.last,
        );
    _scrollToBottom();
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- archivos

  static const _maxImagen = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
  static const _maxVideo = 16 * 1024 * 1024;
  static const _maxDocumento = 100 * 1024 * 1024;

  void _aviso(String texto) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(texto)));
  }

  Future<void> _elegirImagen(ImageSource fuente) async {
    try {
      final x = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: fuente, imageQuality: 80, maxWidth: 2048);
      if (x == null) return;
      await _confirmarEnvio(File(x.path), MessageType.image, x.name);
    } catch (e) {
      _aviso('No se pudo abrir la ${fuente == ImageSource.camera ? 'cámara' : 'galería'}.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _elegirVideo() async {
    try {
      final x = await ImagePicker().pickVideo(source: ImageSource.gallery);
      if (x == null) return;
      await _confirmarEnvio(File(x.path), MessageType.video, x.name);
    } catch (e) {
      _aviso('No se pudo abrir la galería.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _elegirDocumento() async {
    try {
      final r = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(
        type: FileType.custom,
        allowedExtensions: const ['pdf', 'doc', 'docx', 'xls', 'xlsx', 'ppt', 'pptx', 'txt'],
      );
      final path = r?.files.single.path;
      if (path == null) return;
      await _confirmarEnvio(File(path), MessageType.document, r!.files.single.name);
    } catch (e) {
      _aviso('No se pudo abrir el selector de archivos.');
    }
  }

  /// Vista previa con texto opcional antes de enviar
  Future<void> _confirmarEnvio(File file, MessageType type, String nombre) async {
    final tam = await file.length();
    final max = switch (type) {
      MessageType.image => _maxImagen,
      MessageType.video => _maxVideo,
      _ => _maxDocumento,
    };
    if (tam > max) {
      _aviso('El archivo pesa ${(tam / 1048576).toStringAsFixed(1)} MB y WhatsApp admite máximo ${max ~/ 1048576} MB.');
      return;
    }
    if (!mounted) return;

    // El TextEditingController vive dentro del propio sheet: si se destruyera aquí, mientras el sheet
    // todavía se está cerrando, la app se caía al enviar una imagen con texto.
    final texto = await showModalBottomSheet<String>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (ctx) => _VistaPreviaEnvio(file: file, type: type, nombre: nombre, tam: tam),
    );
    if (texto == null) return;

    ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).sendMedia(
          widget.chatId,
          file,
          type,
          caption: texto,
          fileName: nombre,
        );
    _scrollToBottom();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Seguimos usando chatsProvider para obtener los datos del CONTACTO (nombre, avatar, estado)
    final chat = ref.watch(chatsProvider).where((c) => c.id == widget.chatId).firstOrNull;
    if (chat == null) {
      // La lista de chats aún no cargó (o el contacto es nuevo): se muestra el chat con lo que se sabe
      return Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(title: Text(widget.nombre ?? widget.chatId)),
        body: const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }

    // Avisos de error de envío
    ref.listen<String?>(conversationErrorProvider, (_, error) {
      if (error != null) {
        _aviso(error);
        ref.read(conversationErrorProvider.notifier).state = null;
      }
    });
    // Bajar automáticamente cuando llega un mensaje y el usuario está al final
    ref.listen<List<Message>>(conversationProvider, (prev, next) {
      if (prev != null && next.length > prev.length && _nearBottom) _scrollToBottom();
    });
    
    // AGREGADO: Ahora escuchamos los mensajes reales de la API
    final messages = ref.watch(conversationProvider);
    final isLoading = ref.watch(isConversationLoadingProvider);

    
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final isDark = theme.brightness == Brightness.dark;

    final items = _withDateDividers(messages);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor:
          isDark ? AppColors.backgroundDark : const Color(0xFFEDE7DF),
      appBar: AppBar(
        titleSpacing: 0,
        title: InkWell(
          onTap: () => context.push('/chats/${chat.id}/contact'),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              AppAvatar(
                initials: chat.contact.initials,
                colorValue: chat.contact.avatarColorValue,
                isOnline: chat.contact.isOnline,
                radius: 18,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(chat.contact.name,
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                            fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                    Text(
                      chat.contact.isOnline ? 'en línea' : 'últ. vez hoy',
                      style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                        color: chat.contact.isOnline
                            ? AppColors.primary
                            : theme.colorScheme.onSurface
                                .withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        actions: [
          PopupMenuButton<String>(
            itemBuilder: (_) => const [
              PopupMenuItem(value: 'info', child: Text('Ver contacto')),
              PopupMenuItem(value: 'search', child: Text('Buscar')),
              PopupMenuItem(value: 'media', child: Text('Multimedia')),
              // Pendientes por ahora: nota interna, marcar resuelto. La respuesta IA se maneja en "Ver contacto".
              // PopupMenuItem(value: 'note', child: Text('Agregar nota interna')),
              // PopupMenuItem(value: 'resolve', child: Text('Marcar resuelto')),
            ],
            onSelected: (v) {
              if (v == 'info') context.push('/chats/${chat.id}/contact');
              if (v == 'search') {
                Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ChatSearchScreen(chatId: chat.id)));
              }
              if (v == 'media') {
                Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => MediaGalleryScreen(chatId: chat.id, titulo: chat.contact.name)));
              }
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: Center(
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 900),
          child: Column(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            // AGREGADO: Mostrar indicador de carga la primera vez
            child: isLoading && messages.isEmpty
                ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
                : Stack(
                    children: [
                      Positioned.fill(
                        // reverse: la conversación queda anclada al último mensaje aunque las imágenes tarden en cargar
                        child: ListView.builder(
                    controller: _scrollController,
                    reverse: true,
                    cacheExtent: 600,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                    itemCount: items.length,
                    itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                      final item = items[items.length - 1 - i];
                      if (item is DateTime) return _DateDivider(date: item);
                      final m = item as Message;
                      return MessageBubble(
                        message: m,
                        onRetry: m.status == MessageStatus.failed && m.isLocal
                            ? () => ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).retry(widget.chatId, m.id)
                            : null,
                        onDiscard: m.status == MessageStatus.failed && m.isLocal
                            ? () => ref.read(conversationProvider.notifier).discard(m.id)
                            : null,
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                      ),
                      if (_mostrarBajar)
                        Positioned(
                          right: 12,
                          bottom: 12,
                          child: FloatingActionButton.small(
                            heroTag: 'bajar',
                            onPressed: _scrollToBottom,
                            child: const Icon(Icons.keyboard_arrow_down),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
          ),
          _Composer(
            permitido: ref.watch(permisoEnvioProvider),
            controller: _controller,
            onSend: _send,
            onAttach: () => _showAttachSheet(context),
            recording: _grabando,
            seconds: _segundos,
            onRecordStart: _iniciarGrabacion,
            onRecordCancel: () => _terminarGrabacion(enviar: false),
            onRecordSend: () => _terminarGrabacion(enviar: true),
          ),
        ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Interleaves date divider markers between messages.
  List<Object> _withDateDividers(List<Message> messages) {
    final out = <Object>[];
    DateTime? lastDay;
    for (final m in messages) {
      final day = DateTime(m.time.year, m.time.month, m.time.day);
      if (lastDay == null || day != lastDay) {
        out.add(m.time);
        lastDay = day;
      }
      out.add(m);
    }
    return out;
  }

  void _showAttachSheet(BuildContext context) {
    if (!_puedeEnviar) return;
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        void elegir(VoidCallback accion) {
          Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
          accion();
        }

        return SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 24),
            child: Wrap(
              alignment: WrapAlignment.spaceEvenly,
              runSpacing: 20,
              children: [
                _AttachOption(
                  icon: Icons.photo_camera,
                  label: 'Cámara',
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  onTap: () => elegir(() => _elegirImagen(ImageSource.camera)),
                ),
                _AttachOption(
                  icon: Icons.image,
                  label: 'Galería',
                  color: AppColors.blue,
                  onTap: () => elegir(() => _elegirImagen(ImageSource.gallery)),
                ),
                _AttachOption(
                  icon: Icons.videocam,
                  label: 'Video',
                  color: AppColors.aiAccent,
                  onTap: () => elegir(_elegirVideo),
                ),
                _AttachOption(
                  icon: Icons.insert_drive_file,
                  label: 'Documento',
                  color: AppColors.secondary,
                  onTap: () => elegir(_elegirDocumento),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

class _DateDivider extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DateDivider({required this.date});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {

          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface.withValues(alpha: 0.9),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
        ),
        child: Text(
          TimeFormat.dayLabel(date),
          style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Composer extends StatefulWidget {
  const _Composer({
    required this.permitido,
    required this.controller,
    required this.onSend,
    required this.onAttach,
    required this.recording,
    required this.seconds,
    required this.onRecordStart,
    required this.onRecordCancel,
    required this.onRecordSend,
  });
  final bool permitido;
  final TextEditingController controller;
  final VoidCallback onSend;
  final VoidCallback onAttach;
  final bool recording;
  final int seconds;
  final VoidCallback onRecordStart;
  final VoidCallback onRecordCancel;
  final VoidCallback onRecordSend;

  @override
  State<_Composer> createState() => _ComposerState();
}

class _ComposerState extends State<_Composer> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    widget.controller.addListener(_cambio);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    widget.controller.removeListener(_cambio);
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _cambio() => setState(() {});

  String _tiempo(int s) => '${s ~/ 60}:${(s % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final hayTexto = widget.controller.text.trim().isNotEmpty;

    // Fuera de la ventana de 24 h de WhatsApp: texto, adjuntos, cámara y audio quedan inhabilitados
    if (!widget.permitido) {
      final apagado = theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.38);
      return SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, 8, 8, 8),
          color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, 0, 8, 8),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.lock_clock, size: 18, color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'Pasaron más de 24 horas desde el último mensaje del cliente. '
                        'WhatsApp solo permite enviarle una plantilla; hazlo desde la web.',
                        style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                children: [
                  const IconButton(onPressed: null, icon: Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline)),
                  const Expanded(
                    child: TextField(
                      enabled: false,
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        hintText: 'Envío no disponible',
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  CircleAvatar(
                    radius: 24,
                    backgroundColor: apagado.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                    child: Icon(Icons.mic, color: apagado, size: 22),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return SafeArea(
      top: false,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, 8, 8, 8),
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        child: widget.recording
            ? Row(
                children: [
                  IconButton(
                    onPressed: widget.onRecordCancel,
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: AppColors.danger),
                    tooltip: 'Cancelar',
                  ),
                  const Icon(Icons.fiber_manual_record, color: AppColors.danger, size: 14),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(_tiempo(widget.seconds),
                      style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 16)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text('Grabando…',
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
                  ),
                  CircleAvatar(
                    radius: 24,
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                    child: IconButton(
                      onPressed: widget.onRecordSend,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.send, color: Colors.white, size: 20),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              )
            : Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  IconButton(
                      onPressed: widget.onAttach,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add_circle_outline)),
                  Expanded(
                    child: ConstrainedBox(
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 120),
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: widget.controller,
                        minLines: 1,
                        maxLines: 5,
                        textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          hintText: 'Escribe un mensaje',
                          contentPadding:
                              EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 10),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 6),
                  CircleAvatar(
                    radius: 24,
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.primary,
                    child: IconButton(
                      onPressed: hayTexto ? widget.onSend : widget.onRecordStart,
                      icon: Icon(hayTexto ? Icons.send : Icons.mic,
                          color: Colors.white, size: 22),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AttachOption extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AttachOption({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
    required this.onTap,
  });
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {

      width: 80,

        children: [
          InkWell(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),

            child: Container(
              width: 56,
              height: 56,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
              ),
              child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 26),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(label,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Vista previa del archivo con texto opcional antes de enviar. Devuelve el texto (o null si se cancela).
class _VistaPreviaEnvio extends StatefulWidget {
  const _VistaPreviaEnvio({required this.file, required this.type, required this.nombre, required this.tam});
  final File file;
  final MessageType type;
  final String nombre;
  final int tam;

  @override
  State<_VistaPreviaEnvio> createState() => _VistaPreviaEnvioState();
}

class _VistaPreviaEnvioState extends State<_VistaPreviaEnvio> {
  final _caption = TextEditingController();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _caption.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext ctx) {
    final type = widget.type;
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(ctx).bottom + 16),
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  child: type == MessageType.image
                      ? ConstrainedBox(
                          constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.sizeOf(ctx).height * 0.4),
                          child: Image.file(widget.file, fit: BoxFit.contain, cacheWidth: 1200),
                        )
                      : Container(
                          width: double.infinity,
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                          color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.06),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Icon(type == MessageType.video ? Icons.videocam : Icons.insert_drive_file, size: 36),
                              const SizedBox(width: 12),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(widget.nombre,
                                        maxLines: 2,
                                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                        style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                                    Text('${(widget.tam / 1048576).toStringAsFixed(1)} MB',
                                        style: Theme.of(ctx).textTheme.bodySmall),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                TextField(
                  controller: _caption,
                  maxLength: 1024,
                  minLines: 1,
                  maxLines: 3,
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(hintText: 'Agrega un texto (opcional)', counterText: ''),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: OutlinedButton(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                        child: const Text('Cancelar'),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: FilledButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, _caption.text),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.send),
                        label: const Text('Enviar'),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\widgets\document_viewer.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:open_filex/open_filex.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:pdfx/pdfx.dart';

import '../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';

/// Previsualización de documentos: los PDF se ven dentro de la app (con zoom y páginas);
/// el resto de formatos (Word, Excel, PowerPoint, texto) se descargan y se abren con la app instalada.
class DocumentViewer extends StatefulWidget {
  const DocumentViewer({super.key, this.url, this.localPath, required this.fileName});
  final String? url;
  final String? localPath;
  final String fileName;

  @override
  State<DocumentViewer> createState() => _DocumentViewerState();
}

class _DocumentViewerState extends State<DocumentViewer> {
  String? _ruta;
  double? _progreso;
  String? _error;
  PdfController? _pdf;
  int _pagina = 1;
  int _paginas = 0;

  bool get _esPdf => widget.fileName.toLowerCase().endsWith('.pdf');

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _preparar();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pdf?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _preparar() async {
    try {
      final local = widget.localPath;
      String ruta;
      if (local != null && File(local).existsSync()) {
        ruta = local;
      } else {
        ruta = await _descargar(widget.url!);
      }
      if (_esPdf) {
        _pdf = PdfController(document: PdfDocument.openFile(ruta));
      }
      if (mounted) setState(() => _ruta = ruta);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Error preparando documento: $e');
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = 'No se pudo cargar el documento.');
    }
  }

  /// Descarga a la carpeta temporal (queda en caché para abrirlo de nuevo sin red)
  Future<String> _descargar(String url) async {
    final dir = Directory('${(await getTemporaryDirectory()).path}/docs');
    if (!dir.existsSync()) dir.createSync(recursive: true);
    final nombre = '${url.hashCode.abs()}_${widget.fileName.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^A-Za-z0-9._-]'), '_')}';
    final archivo = File('${dir.path}/$nombre');
    if (archivo.existsSync() && archivo.lengthSync() > 0) return archivo.path;

    final cliente = http.Client();
    try {
      final res = await cliente.send(http.Request('GET', Uri.parse(url)));
      if (res.statusCode != 200) throw Exception('HTTP ${res.statusCode}');
      final total = res.contentLength ?? 0;
      var recibido = 0;
      final sink = archivo.openWrite();
      await for (final trozo in res.stream) {
        sink.add(trozo);
        recibido += trozo.length;
        if (total > 0 && mounted) setState(() => _progreso = recibido / total);
      }
      await sink.close();
      return archivo.path;
    } catch (e) {
      if (archivo.existsSync()) archivo.deleteSync();
      rethrow;
    } finally {
      cliente.close();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _abrirExterno() async {
    final r = _ruta;
    if (r == null) return;
    final res = await OpenFilex.open(r);
    if (res.type != ResultType.done && mounted) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('No hay una app instalada para abrir este tipo de archivo.')),
      );
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.fileName, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16)),
        actions: [
          if (_ruta != null)
            IconButton(onPressed: _abrirExterno, icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new), tooltip: 'Abrir con otra app'),
        ],
      ),
      body: _error != null
          ? Center(child: Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24), child: Text(_error!, textAlign: TextAlign.center)))
          : _ruta == null
              ? Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      CircularProgressIndicator(value: _progreso),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Text(_progreso == null ? 'Descargando…' : '${(_progreso! * 100).toStringAsFixed(0)} %'),
                    ],
                  ),
                )
              : _esPdf && _pdf != null
                  ? Stack(
                      children: [
                        PdfView(
                          controller: _pdf!,
                          onDocumentLoaded: (doc) => setState(() => _paginas = doc.pagesCount),
                          onPageChanged: (p) => setState(() => _pagina = p),
                        ),
                        if (_paginas > 0)
                          Positioned(
                            bottom: 16,
                            right: 16,
                            child: Container(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black54, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
                              child: Text('$_pagina / $_paginas', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    )
                  : Center(
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          const Icon(Icons.insert_drive_file, size: 72, color: AppColors.secondary),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          Text(widget.fileName, textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                          const SizedBox(height: 16),
                          FilledButton.icon(
                            onPressed: _abrirExterno,
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new),
                            label: const Text('Abrir'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
    );
  }
}

```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\widgets\message_bubble.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:gmb/core/services/cache_service.dart';
import 'package:just_audio/just_audio.dart';
import 'video_viewer.dart';
import 'document_viewer.dart';
import 'video_thumb.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

import '../../../core/models/message.dart';
import '../../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/time_format.dart';

/// Renders a single chat message of any supported type.
class MessageBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  const MessageBubble({
    super.key,
    required this.message,
    this.onRetry,
    this.onDiscard,
  });

  final Message message;

  /// Solo para mensajes locales que fallaron al enviarse
  final VoidCallback? onRetry;
  final VoidCallback? onDiscard;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isDark = Theme.of(context).brightness == Brightness.dark;

    // Internal notes render full-width and centered.
    if (message.type == MessageType.note) {
      return _NoteCard(message: message);
    }

    final outgoing = message.isOutgoing;
    final bg = outgoing
        ? (isDark ? AppColors.bubbleOutDark : AppColors.bubbleOut)
        : (isDark ? AppColors.bubbleInDark : AppColors.bubbleIn);
    final textColor = isDark ? AppColors.textPrimaryDark : AppColors.textPrimary;

    return Align(
      alignment: outgoing ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 3, horizontal: 8),
        constraints: BoxConstraints(
          maxWidth: (MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width * 0.78).clamp(0.0, 560.0),
        ),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: bg,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: const Radius.circular(14),
            topRight: const Radius.circular(14),
            bottomLeft: Radius.circular(outgoing ? 14 : 4),
            bottomRight: Radius.circular(outgoing ? 4 : 14),
          ),
          border: Border.all(
            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.outline.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            _SourceTag(source: message.source),
            _content(context, textColor),
            // Los audios manejan su propio footer con el estado
            if (message.type != MessageType.audio) 
              _footer(context, textColor),
            if (message.status == MessageStatus.failed && message.isLocal)
              _failedBar(context),
            if (message.reactions.isNotEmpty) _reactions(),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _content(BuildContext context, Color textColor) {
    switch (message.type) {
      case MessageType.text:
        return Text(message.text ?? '',
            style: TextStyle(color: textColor, height: 1.35));
      case MessageType.image:
        return _MediaPlaceholder(
          icon: Icons.image,
          label: 'Imagen',
          caption: message.caption,
          aspect: 1.3,
          imageUrl: message.urlArchivo,
          localPath: message.localPath,
          sending: message.status == MessageStatus.sending,
          onTap: () => _openImage(context),
        );
      case MessageType.video:
        return _MediaPlaceholder(
          icon: Icons.play_circle_fill,
          label: 'Video',
          caption: message.caption,
          aspect: 1.6,
          showPlay: true,
          imageUrl: null, // la portada se genera aparte (VideoThumb)
          videoUrl: message.urlArchivo,
          videoLocal: message.localPath,
          sending: message.status == MessageStatus.sending,
          onTap: (message.urlArchivo == null && message.localPath == null)
              ? null
              : () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => VideoViewer(url: message.urlArchivo, localPath: message.localPath),
                  )),
        );
      case MessageType.document:
      case MessageType.pdf:
        return _FileTile(message: message, textColor: textColor);
      case MessageType.audio:
        return _AudioTile(message: message, textColor: textColor); // MEJORADO
      case MessageType.location:
        return _LocationTile(message: message, textColor: textColor);
      case MessageType.buttons:
        return _ButtonsContent(message: message, textColor: textColor);
      case MessageType.list:
        return _ListContent(message: message, textColor: textColor);
      case MessageType.form:
        return _FormContent(message: message, textColor: textColor);
       case MessageType.template:
        return _TemplateContent(message: message, textColor: textColor); // NUEVO
      case MessageType.note:
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
  }

  void _openImage(BuildContext context) {
    final url = message.urlArchivo;
    final local = message.localPath;
    if (url == null && local == null) return;
    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
      fullscreenDialog: true,
      builder: (_) => Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        appBar: AppBar(backgroundColor: Colors.black, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
        body: Center(
          child: InteractiveViewer(
            maxScale: 5,
            child: url == null
                ? Image.file(File(local!))
                : Image.network(url, errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) =>
                    const Icon(Icons.broken_image, color: Colors.white54, size: 64)),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ));
  }

  Widget _failedBar(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          if (message.errorText != null)
            Text(message.errorText!,
                textAlign: TextAlign.end,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: AppColors.danger)),
          Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              if (onDiscard != null)
                TextButton(onPressed: onDiscard, child: const Text('Descartar')),
              if (onRetry != null)
                FilledButton.tonalIcon(
                  onPressed: onRetry,
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 18),
                  label: const Text('Reintentar'),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _footer(BuildContext context, Color textColor) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Text(
            TimeFormat.clock(message.time),
            style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11, color: textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
          ),
          if (message.isOutgoing) ...[
            const SizedBox(width: 4),
            Icon(
              message.status == MessageStatus.read ||
                      message.status == MessageStatus.delivered
                  ? Icons.done_all
                  : message.status == MessageStatus.sending
                      ? Icons.schedule
                  : message.status == MessageStatus.failed
                      ? Icons.error_outline
                      : Icons.done,
              size: 15,
              color: message.status == MessageStatus.read
                  ? AppColors.blue
                  : message.status == MessageStatus.failed
                      ? AppColors.danger
                      : textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _reactions() {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
      child: Wrap(
        spacing: 4,
        children: message.reactions
            .map((r) => Container(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.06),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: Text(r.emoji, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13)),
                ))
            .toList(),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SourceTag extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SourceTag({required this.source});
  final MessageSource source;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final (label, color, icon) = switch (source) {
      MessageSource.ai => ('IA', AppColors.aiAccent, Icons.auto_awesome),
      MessageSource.chatbot =>
        ('Chatbot', AppColors.botAccent, Icons.smart_toy),
      MessageSource.n8n => ('n8n', AppColors.n8nAccent, Icons.account_tree),
      _ => (null, AppColors.primary, Icons.person),
    };
    if (label == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 13, color: color),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          Text(label,
              style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: color)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
