```python
class _MediaPlaceholder extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MediaPlaceholder({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.aspect,
    this.caption,
    this.showPlay = false,
    this.imageUrl,
    this.localPath,
    this.videoUrl,
    this.videoLocal,
    this.sending = false,
    this.onTap,
  });
  final String? videoUrl;
  final String? videoLocal;
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final double aspect;
  final String? caption;
  final bool showPlay;
  final String? imageUrl;
  final String? localPath;
  final bool sending;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        GestureDetector(
          onTap: onTap,
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            child: AspectRatio(
              aspectRatio: aspect,
              child: Stack(
                fit: StackFit.expand,
                children: [
                  if (imageUrl != null)
                    _buildRealImage()
                  else if (localPath != null && !showPlay)
                    Image.file(File(localPath!), fit: BoxFit.cover)
                  else if (showPlay && (videoUrl != null || videoLocal != null))
                    VideoThumb(url: videoUrl, localPath: videoLocal, fallback: _buildPlaceholder(context))
                  else
                    _buildPlaceholder(context),
                  if (showPlay && imageUrl == null)
                    Container(
                      color: Colors.black26,
                      child: const Center(
                        child: Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, size: 50, color: Colors.white70),
                      ),
                    ),
                  if (sending)
                    Container(
                      color: Colors.black38,
                      child: const Center(
                        child: SizedBox(
                          width: 32,
                          height: 32,
                          child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 3, color: Colors.white),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (caption != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(caption!, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 14, height: 1.3)),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRealImage() {
    return Stack(
      fit: StackFit.expand,
      children: [
        Image.network(
          imageUrl!,
          fit: BoxFit.cover,
          cacheWidth: 700, // decodifica en pequeño: chats largos con muchas fotos no saturan la memoria
          gaplessPlayback: true,
          loadingBuilder: (context, child, loadingProgress) {
            if (loadingProgress == null) return child;
            return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2));
          },
          errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) {
            final local = localPath;
            if (local != null && File(local).existsSync()) {
              return Image.file(File(local), fit: BoxFit.cover);
            }
            return _buildPlaceholder(context);
          },
        ),
        if (showPlay)
          Container(
            color: Colors.black26,
            child: const Center(
              child: Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, size: 50, color: Colors.white70),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPlaceholder(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: 240,
      color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08),
      child: Center(
        child: Icon(icon, size: 48, color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.4)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// --- MEJORADO: Íconos dinámicos como en tu Laravel ---
class _FileTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FileTile({required this.message, required this.textColor});
  final Message message;
  final Color textColor;

  // Lógica de íconos idéntica a tu Blade (str_contains)
  IconData _getFileIcon(String? fileName) {
    if (fileName == null) return Icons.insert_drive_file;
    final lower = fileName.toLowerCase();
    if (lower.endsWith('.pdf')) return Icons.picture_as_pdf;
    if (lower.endsWith('.doc') || lower.endsWith('.docx')) return Icons.description;
    if (lower.endsWith('.xls') || lower.endsWith('.xlsx')) return Icons.table_chart;
    if (lower.endsWith('.ppt') || lower.endsWith('.pptx')) return Icons.slideshow;
    if (lower.endsWith('.zip') || lower.endsWith('.rar')) return Icons.folder_zip;
    return Icons.insert_drive_file;
  }

  Color _getFileColor(String? fileName) {
    if (fileName == null) return AppColors.blue;
    final lower = fileName.toLowerCase();
    if (lower.endsWith('.pdf')) return AppColors.danger;
    if (lower.endsWith('.doc') || lower.endsWith('.docx')) return const Color(0xFF4CAF50); // Success Green
    if (lower.endsWith('.xls') || lower.endsWith('.xlsx')) return AppColors.blue;
    if (lower.endsWith('.ppt') || lower.endsWith('.pptx')) return const Color(0xFFFF9800); // Warning Orange
    if (lower.endsWith('.zip') || lower.endsWith('.rar')) return Colors.cyan;
    return AppColors.blue;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final fileName = message.fileName ?? (message.urlArchivo?.split('/').last ?? 'Documento');
    final icon = _getFileIcon(fileName);
    final color = _getFileColor(fileName);

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          onTap: (message.urlArchivo == null && message.localPath == null)
              ? null
              : () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => DocumentViewer(
                      url: message.urlArchivo,
                      localPath: message.localPath,
                      fileName: fileName,
                    ),
                  )),
          child: Container(
          width: 240,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 28),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  fileName,
                  maxLines: 2,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(color: textColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600, fontSize: 14),
                ),
              ),
              message.status == MessageStatus.sending
                  ? const SizedBox(width: 20, height: 20, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                  : Icon(Icons.download_rounded, color: textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        ),
        if (message.caption != null) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(message.caption!, style: TextStyle(color: textColor, fontSize: 14)),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}

// --- MEJORADO: Visual de Onda de Audio como en Laravel ---
class _AudioTile extends StatefulWidget {
  const _AudioTile({required this.message, required this.textColor});
  final Message message;
  final Color textColor;

  @override
  State<_AudioTile> createState() => _AudioTileState();
}

class _AudioTileState extends State<_AudioTile> {
  final AudioPlayer _player = AudioPlayer();
  final CacheService _cacheService = CacheService();
  bool _isPlaying = false;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  Duration _duration = Duration.zero;
  Duration _position = Duration.zero;

  final List<double> _waveHeights = const [
    0.4, 0.7, 0.5, 0.9, 0.6, 0.8, 0.45, 0.75, 0.55, 0.85,
    0.5, 0.7, 0.4, 0.65, 0.55, 0.75, 0.45, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5
  ];

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _player.playerStateStream.listen((state) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _isPlaying = state.playing);
      if (state.processingState == ProcessingState.completed) {
        _player.seek(Duration.zero);
        _player.pause();
      }
    });
    _player.durationStream.listen((d) {
      if (d != null && mounted) setState(() => _duration = d);
    });
    _player.positionStream.listen((p) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _position = p);
    });
  }

  Future<void> _togglePlay() async {
    try {
      if (_player.audioSource == null) {
        setState(() => _isLoading = true);
        final local = widget.message.localPath;
        final path = widget.message.urlArchivo;
        if (local != null && path == null) {
          await _player.setFilePath(local);
        } else {
          if (path == null) {
            setState(() => _isLoading = false);
            return;
          }
          String fullUrl = path;
          if (!path.startsWith('http')) {
            final server = await _cacheService.getServerUrl();
            fullUrl = '$server$path';
          }
          await _player.setUrl(fullUrl);
        }
        setState(() => _isLoading = false);
      }

      if (_isPlaying) {
        await _player.pause();
      } else {
        await _player.play();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Error reproduciendo audio: $e');
      setState(() => _isLoading = false);
    }
  }

  String _formatDuration(Duration d) {
    final minutes = d.inMinutes.remainder(60).toString();
    final seconds = d.inSeconds.remainder(60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    return '$minutes:$seconds';
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _player.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final progress = _duration.inMilliseconds > 0
        ? _position.inMilliseconds / _duration.inMilliseconds
        : 0.0;

    final ancho = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width * 0.6;
    return SizedBox(
      width: ancho < 260 ? ancho : 260,
      child: Row(
        children: [
          InkWell(
            onTap: _isLoading ? null : _togglePlay,
            customBorder: const CircleBorder(),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                color: AppColors.secondary.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
              ),
              child: _isLoading
                  ? const SizedBox(
                      width: 24,
                      height: 24,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                    )
                  : Icon(
                      _isPlaying ? Icons.pause : Icons.play_arrow,
                      size: 28,
                      color: AppColors.secondary,
                    ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  height: 30,
                  child: Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: _waveHeights.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
                      final played = (entry.key / _waveHeights.length) <= progress;
                      return Container(
                        width: 3,
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 1),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: (played ? AppColors.secondary : widget.textColor)
                              .withValues(alpha: played ? 0.9 : 0.5),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                        ),
                        height: 30 * entry.value,
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  (_isPlaying || _position > Duration.zero)
                      ? _formatDuration(_position)
                      : (widget.message.duration ?? '0:00'),
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: widget.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          if (widget.message.isOutgoing)
            Icon(
              switch (widget.message.status) {
                MessageStatus.sending => Icons.schedule,
                MessageStatus.failed => Icons.error_outline,
                MessageStatus.sent => Icons.done,
                _ => Icons.done_all,
              },
              size: 16,
              color: widget.message.status == MessageStatus.read
                  ? AppColors.blue
                  : widget.message.status == MessageStatus.failed
                      ? AppColors.danger
                      : widget.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
            )
          else
            Icon(Icons.mic_none, color: widget.textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6), size: 22),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// --- MEJORADO: Simula el "Ver Respuesta Formulario" de tu Laravel ---
class _FormContent extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FormContent({required this.message, required this.textColor});
  final Message message;
  final Color textColor;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        if (message.text != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
            child: Text(message.text!, style: TextStyle(color: textColor, height: 1.35)),
          ),
        // Si no hay texto, mostramos la tarjeta genérica de Formulario (como tu btnVerFormulario)
        if (message.text == null)
          Container(
            width: 250,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.n8nAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.file_copy_outlined, color: AppColors.n8nAccent, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Text('Ver Respuesta Formulario', style: TextStyle(color: textColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        // Si hay acciones (campos del form)
        if (message.actions.isNotEmpty)
          Container(
            width: 250,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.n8nAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: message.actions
                  .map((a) => Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
                        child: Row(
                          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                          children: [
                            Text(a.label, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: textColor.withValues(alpha: 0.6))),
                            Flexible(
                              child: Text(a.description ?? '', textAlign: TextAlign.end, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, color: textColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ))
                  .toList(),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// --- NUEVO: Dibuja Plantillas de WhatsApp (Header + Texto + Botones) ---
class _TemplateContent extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TemplateContent({required this.message, required this.textColor});
  final Message message;
  final Color textColor;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        // 1. HEADER (Imagen, Video o Documento)
        if (message.templateHeaderUrl != null) ...[
          _buildHeader(context),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
        ],
        
        // 2. TEXTO (Body)
        if (message.text != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
            child: Text(message.text!, style: TextStyle(color: textColor, height: 1.35)),
          ),

        // 3. BOTONES
        if (message.actions.isNotEmpty)
          ...message.actions.map((a) => Container(
                width: 240,
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      a.description == 'Teléfono' ? Icons.phone_outlined : Icons.open_in_new,
                      size: 18, 
                      color: AppColors.secondary,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(a.label, style: TextStyle(color: textColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              )),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildHeader(BuildContext context) {
    switch (message.templateHeaderType) {
      case MessageType.image:
        return ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          child: Image.network(
            message.templateHeaderUrl!,
            fit: BoxFit.cover,
            loadingBuilder: (context, child, progress) {
              if (progress == null) return child;
              return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2));
            },
            errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const Icon(Icons.broken_image, size: 50),
          ),
        );
      case MessageType.video:
        // En plantillas, el "video" suele ser solo una imagen de portada
        return ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          child: Stack(
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            children: [
              Image.network(message.templateHeaderUrl!, fit: BoxFit.cover, errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => const SizedBox()),
              Container(color: Colors.black26, child: const Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, size: 50, color: Colors.white70)),
            ],
          ),
        );
      case MessageType.document:
        // Reutilizamos la lógica de archivo
        return _FileTile(
          message: Message(
            id: '', 
            type: MessageType.document, 
            source: MessageSource.agent, 
            time: DateTime.now(), 
            urlArchivo: message.templateHeaderUrl,
            fileName: message.templateHeaderUrl?.split('/').last,
          ), 
          textColor: textColor
        );
      default:
        return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
  }
}

/// Abre un archivo (documento, video) con la app del teléfono o el navegador.
Future<void> _openExternal(BuildContext context, String url) async {
  final ok = await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
  if (!ok && context.mounted) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(content: Text('No se pudo abrir el archivo.')),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\widgets\video_thumb.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
import 'package:video_thumbnail_gdx_plus/video_thumbnail_gdx_plus.dart';

/// Portada (primer cuadro) de un video, generada en el teléfono y guardada en caché.
class VideoThumb extends StatelessWidget {
  const VideoThumb({super.key, this.url, this.localPath, this.fallback, this.fit = BoxFit.cover});
  final String? url;
  final String? localPath;
  final Widget? fallback;
  final BoxFit fit;

  static final Map<String, Future<String?>> _cache = {};

  Future<String?> _generar() {
    final origen = localPath ?? url!;
    return _cache.putIfAbsent(origen, () async {
      try {
        final dir = (await getTemporaryDirectory()).path;
        return await VideoThumbnail.thumbnailFile(
          video: origen,
          thumbnailPath: dir,
          imageFormat: ImageFormat.JPEG,
          maxWidth: 480,
          quality: 60,
        );
      } catch (e) {
        debugPrint('⚠️ No se pudo generar la portada del video: $e');
        return null;
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (url == null && localPath == null) return fallback ?? const SizedBox.shrink();
    return FutureBuilder<String?>(
      future: _generar(),
      builder: (_, snap) {
        final ruta = snap.data;
        if (ruta == null) return fallback ?? const ColoredBox(color: Colors.black12);
        return Image.file(File(ruta), fit: fit, gaplessPlayback: true);
      },
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\chats\widgets\video_viewer.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import 'package:video_player/video_player.dart';

import 'video_thumb.dart';

/// Reproductor de video a pantalla completa (desde la URL del servidor o el archivo local).
class VideoViewer extends StatefulWidget {
  const VideoViewer({super.key, this.url, this.localPath});
  final String? url;
  final String? localPath;

  @override
  State<VideoViewer> createState() => _VideoViewerState();
}

class _VideoViewerState extends State<VideoViewer> {
  VideoPlayerController? _c;
  bool _error = false;
  String _detalle = '';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _iniciar();
  }

  Future<void> _iniciar() async {
    try {
      final url = widget.url;
      final local = widget.localPath;
      final c = (url != null)
          ? VideoPlayerController.networkUrl(Uri.parse(url))
          : VideoPlayerController.file(File(local!));
      _c = c;
      await c.initialize();
      c.addListener(() {
        if (mounted) setState(() {});
      });
      await c.play();
      if (mounted) setState(() {});
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('❌ Error reproduciendo video (${widget.url}): $e');
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = true;
          _detalle = e.toString();
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _c?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = _c;
    final listo = c != null && c.value.isInitialized;
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.black,
      appBar: AppBar(backgroundColor: Colors.black, foregroundColor: Colors.white),
      body: Center(
        child: _error
            ? Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Text('No se pudo reproducir el video aquí.',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 16)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(_detalle,
                        maxLines: 4,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12)),
                    if (widget.url != null) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      FilledButton.icon(
                        onPressed: () => launchUrl(Uri.parse(widget.url!), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication),
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new),
                        label: const Text('Abrir en otra app'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              )
            : !listo
                ? Stack(
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Positioned.fill(
                        child: Opacity(
                          opacity: 0.5,
                          child: VideoThumb(url: widget.url, localPath: widget.localPath, fit: BoxFit.contain),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white),
                          SizedBox(height: 12),
                          Text('Cargando video…', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  )
                : Column(
                    children: [
                      // Expanded + Center: el video se ajusta al alto disponible (los verticales no desbordan)
                      Expanded(
                        child: Center(
                          child: AspectRatio(
                            aspectRatio: c.value.aspectRatio == 0 ? 16 / 9 : c.value.aspectRatio,
                            child: GestureDetector(
                              onTap: () => c.value.isPlaying ? c.pause() : c.play(),
                              child: Stack(
                                alignment: Alignment.center,
                                children: [
                                  VideoPlayer(c),
                                  if (!c.value.isPlaying)
                                    const Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, size: 64, color: Colors.white70),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                        child: VideoProgressIndicator(c, allowScrubbing: true),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\contacts\contact_info_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../core/models/message.dart';
import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import '../../widgets/section_card.dart';
import '../chats/chat_search_screen.dart';
import 'media_gallery_screen.dart';

class ContactInfoScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ContactInfoScreen({super.key, required this.chatId});
  final String chatId;

  @override
  ConsumerState<ContactInfoScreen> createState() => _ContactInfoScreenState();
}

class _ContactInfoScreenState extends ConsumerState<ContactInfoScreen> {
  final _api = ApiService();

  bool? _chatbot; // null = cargando
  bool? _ia;
  bool _guardando = false;
  List<Message> _media = [];
  bool _mediaCargando = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cargarDetalle();
    _cargarMedia();
  }

  Future<void> _cargarDetalle() async {
    try {
      final d = await _api.getContactoDetalle(widget.chatId);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _chatbot = d['chatbot_activo'] == true;
        _ia = d['ia_activa'] == true;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('⚠️ Detalle del contacto: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cargarMedia() async {
    try {
      final r = await _api.getMultimedia(widget.chatId, tipo: 'media', limit: 12);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _media = r);
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _mediaCargando = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cambiar(String campo, bool valor) async {
    if (_guardando) return;
    final anterior = campo == 'ia' ? _ia : _chatbot;
    setState(() {
      _guardando = true;
      if (campo == 'ia') {
        _ia = valor;
      } else {
        _chatbot = valor;
      }
    });
    try {
      await _api.setAutomatizacion(widget.chatId, campo: campo, activo: valor);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        if (campo == 'ia') {
          _ia = anterior;
        } else {
          _chatbot = anterior;
        }
      });
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('$e')));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _guardando = false);
    }
  }

  void _buscar() => Navigator.of(context).push(
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => ChatSearchScreen(chatId: widget.chatId)),
      );

  void _verMultimedia(String nombre) => Navigator.of(context).push(
        MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => MediaGalleryScreen(chatId: widget.chatId, titulo: nombre)),
      );

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final chat = ref.watch(chatsProvider).firstWhere((c) => c.id == widget.chatId);
    final c = chat.contact;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      body: CustomScrollView(
        slivers: [
          SliverAppBar(
            pinned: true,
            expandedHeight: 220,
            flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
              background: Container(
                color: AppColors.secondary,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 60, bottom: 16),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    AppAvatar(
                      initials: c.initials,
                      colorValue: c.avatarColorValue,
                      radius: 44,
                      isOnline: c.isOnline,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Text(c.name,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 2),
                    Text(c.phone, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.85))),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          SliverPadding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            sliver: SliverList.list(children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly,
                children: [
                  _accion(Icons.message, 'Mensaje', () => Navigator.of(context).pop()),
                  _accion(Icons.search, 'Buscar', _buscar),
                  _accion(Icons.perm_media_outlined, 'Multimedia', () => _verMultimedia(c.name)),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              const SectionHeader(title: 'Información'),
              GroupedCard(children: [
                AppTile(icon: Icons.phone_outlined, title: c.phone, subtitle: 'Teléfono'),
              ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              const SectionHeader(title: 'Respuestas automáticas'),
              GroupedCard(children: [
                AppTile(
                  icon: Icons.smart_toy_outlined,
                  iconColor: AppColors.aiAccent,
                  title: 'Respuestas con IA',
                  subtitle: 'El asistente responde a este contacto',
                  trailing: _interruptor(_ia, (v) => _cambiar('ia', v)),
                ),
                AppTile(
                  icon: Icons.account_tree_outlined,
                  iconColor: AppColors.aiAccent,
                  title: 'Chatbot de nodos',
                  subtitle: 'Flujo automático de respuestas',
                  trailing: _interruptor(_chatbot, (v) => _cambiar('chatbot', v)),
                ),
              ]),
              // TODO: "Agente asignado" y "Administrar etiquetas" (pendiente de conectar con el servidor)
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              SectionHeader(title: 'Multimedia compartida', action: 'Ver todo', onAction: () => _verMultimedia(c.name)),
              _tira(theme, c.name),
            ]),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _interruptor(bool? valor, ValueChanged<bool> alCambiar) {
    if (valor == null) {
      return const SizedBox(width: 24, height: 24, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2));
    }
    return Switch(value: valor, onChanged: _guardando ? null : alCambiar);
  }

  Widget _tira(ThemeData theme, String nombre) {
    if (_mediaCargando) {
      return const SizedBox(height: 80, child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2)));
    }
    if (_media.isEmpty) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
        child: Text('Aún no hay fotos ni videos', style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
      );
    }
    return SizedBox(
      height: 90,
      child: ListView.separated(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: _media.length,
        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 6),
        itemBuilder: (_, i) => SizedBox(
          width: 90,
          child: ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            child: MediaThumb(mensaje: _media[i], onTap: () => abrirMultimedia(context, _media, i)),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _accion(IconData icon, String label, VoidCallback onTap) {
    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        IconButton.filledTonal(onPressed: onTap, icon: Icon(icon)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12)),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\contacts\media_gallery_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/message.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../core/utils/time_format.dart';
import '../chats/widgets/document_viewer.dart';
import '../chats/widgets/video_thumb.dart';
import '../chats/widgets/video_viewer.dart';

/// Multimedia compartida del chat, como en WhatsApp: pestañas Multimedia (fotos y videos) y Documentos.
class MediaGalleryScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const MediaGalleryScreen({super.key, required this.chatId, required this.titulo});
  final String chatId;
  final String titulo;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return DefaultTabController(
      length: 2,
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: Text(titulo, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          bottom: const TabBar(tabs: [Tab(text: 'Multimedia'), Tab(text: 'Documentos')]),
        ),
        body: TabBarView(
          children: [
            _GaleriaTab(chatId: chatId, tipo: 'media'),
            _GaleriaTab(chatId: chatId, tipo: 'documentos'),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _GaleriaTab extends StatefulWidget {
  const _GaleriaTab({required this.chatId, required this.tipo});
  final String chatId;
  final String tipo;

  @override
  State<_GaleriaTab> createState() => _GaleriaTabState();
}

class _GaleriaTabState extends State<_GaleriaTab> with AutomaticKeepAliveClientMixin {
  final _api = ApiService();
  final _scroll = ScrollController();
  final List<Message> _items = [];
  bool _cargando = true;
  bool _cargandoMas = false;
  bool _fin = false;
  String? _error;

  @override
  bool get wantKeepAlive => true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _scroll.addListener(() {
      if (_scroll.position.pixels > _scroll.position.maxScrollExtent - 400) _cargarMas();
    });
    _cargar();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scroll.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _cargar() async {
    setState(() {
      _cargando = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final r = await _api.getMultimedia(widget.chatId, tipo: widget.tipo);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items
          ..clear()
          ..addAll(r);
        _fin = r.length < 60;
        _cargando = false;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = 'No se pudo cargar. Revisa tu conexión.';
          _cargando = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cargarMas() async {
    if (_cargandoMas || _fin || _items.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() => _cargandoMas = true);
    try {
      final r = await _api.getMultimedia(widget.chatId, tipo: widget.tipo, beforeId: _items.last.id);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _items.addAll(r);
        _fin = r.length < 60;
        _cargandoMas = false;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _cargandoMas = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    super.build(context);
    if (_cargando) return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    if (_error != null) {
      return Center(
        child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Text(_error!),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          FilledButton(onPressed: _cargar, child: const Text('Reintentar')),
        ]),
      );
    }
    if (_items.isEmpty) {
      return Center(
        child: Text(widget.tipo == 'media' ? 'Aún no hay fotos ni videos' : 'Aún no hay documentos'),
      );
    }
    return widget.tipo == 'media' ? _cuadricula() : _lista();
  }

  Widget _cuadricula() {
    return GridView.builder(
      controller: _scroll,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
      gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
        crossAxisCount: 3,
        mainAxisSpacing: 2,
        crossAxisSpacing: 2,
      ),
      itemCount: _items.length,
      itemBuilder: (_, i) => MediaThumb(
        mensaje: _items[i],
        onTap: () => abrirMultimedia(context, _items, i),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _lista() {
    return ListView.separated(
      controller: _scroll,
      itemCount: _items.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const Divider(height: 1),
      itemBuilder: (_, i) {
        final m = _items[i];
        final nombre = m.fileName ?? m.caption ?? 'Documento';
        return ListTile(
          leading: const Icon(Icons.insert_drive_file_outlined),
          title: Text(nombre, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          subtitle: Text([if (m.fileSize != null) m.fileSize!, TimeFormat.dayLabel(m.time)].join(' · ')),
          onTap: m.urlArchivo == null
              ? null
              : () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
                    builder: (_) => DocumentViewer(url: m.urlArchivo, fileName: nombre),
                  )),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

/// Miniatura cuadrada de una foto o un video
class MediaThumb extends StatelessWidget {
  const MediaThumb({super.key, required this.mensaje, required this.onTap});
  final Message mensaje;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final esVideo = mensaje.type == MessageType.video;
    final url = mensaje.urlArchivo;
    final fondo = Container(
      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
      child: Icon(esVideo ? Icons.videocam_outlined : Icons.image_outlined),
    );

    Widget imagen;
    if (url == null) {
      imagen = fondo;
    } else if (esVideo) {
      imagen = VideoThumb(url: url, fallback: fondo);
    } else {
      imagen = Image.network(
        url,
        fit: BoxFit.cover,
        cacheWidth: 360,
        errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => fondo,
      );
    }

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Stack(
        fit: StackFit.expand,
        children: [
          imagen,
          if (esVideo)
            const Center(child: Icon(Icons.play_circle_fill, color: Colors.white, size: 30)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Abre la foto o el video tocado; las fotos se pueden pasar con el dedo (solo las fotos de la lista).
void abrirMultimedia(BuildContext context, List<Message> lista, int indice) {
  final m = lista[indice];
  if (m.urlArchivo == null) return;

  if (m.type == MessageType.video) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => VideoViewer(url: m.urlArchivo)));
    return;
  }

  final fotos = lista.where((x) => x.type == MessageType.image && x.urlArchivo != null).toList();
  final inicio = fotos.indexWhere((x) => x.id == m.id);
  Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute(
    fullscreenDialog: true,
    builder: (_) => _VisorFotos(fotos: fotos, inicio: inicio < 0 ? 0 : inicio),
  ));
}

class _VisorFotos extends StatefulWidget {
  const _VisorFotos({required this.fotos, required this.inicio});
  final List<Message> fotos;
  final int inicio;

  @override
  State<_VisorFotos> createState() => _VisorFotosState();
}

class _VisorFotosState extends State<_VisorFotos> {
  late final PageController _page = PageController(initialPage: widget.inicio);
  late int _actual = widget.inicio;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _page.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final m = widget.fotos[_actual];
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.black,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        title: Text(TimeFormat.dayLabel(m.time), style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 15)),
      ),
      body: PageView.builder(
        controller: _page,
        itemCount: widget.fotos.length,
        onPageChanged: (i) => setState(() => _actual = i),
        itemBuilder: (_, i) => InteractiveViewer(
          maxScale: 5,
          child: Center(
            child: Image.network(
              widget.fotos[i].urlArchivo!,
              errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) =>
                  const Icon(Icons.broken_image_outlined, color: Colors.white54, size: 64),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\dashboard\home_dashboard_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/providers/user_provider.dart';
import '../../core/router/app_router.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../core/utils/responsive.dart';
import '../../core/utils/time_format.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import '../../widgets/section_card.dart';
import '../../widgets/stat_card.dart';

class HomeDashboardScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const HomeDashboardScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    // Obtener el estado del usuario (ya debería estar en 'success')
    final userState = ref.watch(userProvider);

    // Cargar datos si es la primera vez
    if (userState.status == ProfileStatus.initial) {
      Future.microtask(() => ref.read(userProvider.notifier).loadProfile());
    }

    final user = userState.user;
    // Calcular iniciales de forma segura (siempre tendrá un valor por defecto)
    String initials = 'GJ';
    if (user != null) {
      // Si tiene empresa, usa las iniciales de la empresa, si no, del usuario
      final source = user.empresa?.nombre ?? user.nombreCompleto;
      final parts = source.split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
      if (parts.isNotEmpty) {
        initials = parts.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();
      }
    }

    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final chats = ref.watch(chatsProvider);
    final unread = ref.watch(unreadNotificationsProvider);
    final resumen = ref.watch(resumenProvider);
    final crossAxisCount = Responsive.value(context,
        mobile: 2, tablet: 3, desktop: 4);

    return Scaffold(
      body: SafeArea(
        child: RefreshIndicator(
          onRefresh: () async {
            ref.invalidate(resumenProvider);
            await Future.wait([
              ref.read(chatsProvider.notifier).loadChats(),
              ref.read(userProvider.notifier).refreshProfile(),
            ]);
          },
          child: CustomScrollView(
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          slivers: [
            SliverPadding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                Responsive.horizontalPadding(context),
                16,
                Responsive.horizontalPadding(context),
                100,
              ),
              sliver: SliverList.list(children: [
                _header(context, theme, unread, initials, user),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                resumen.when(
                  data: (r) => GridView.count(
                    crossAxisCount: crossAxisCount,
                    shrinkWrap: true,
                    physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                    mainAxisSpacing: 12,
                    crossAxisSpacing: 12,
                    childAspectRatio: 1.15,
                    children: [
                      StatCard(
                        icon: Icons.forum_rounded,
                        value: '${r['conversaciones_activas'] ?? 0}',
                        label: 'Conversaciones activas (24 h)',
                        color: AppColors.primary,
                      ),
                      StatCard(
                        icon: Icons.mark_chat_unread_rounded,
                        value: '${r['mensajes_sin_leer'] ?? 0}',
                        label: 'Mensajes sin leer (${r['chats_sin_leer'] ?? 0} chats)',
                        color: AppColors.warning,
                      ),
                      StatCard(
                        icon: Icons.call_received_rounded,
                        value: '${r['recibidos_hoy'] ?? 0}',
                        label: 'Mensajes recibidos hoy',
                        color: AppColors.blue,
                      ),
                      StatCard(
                        icon: Icons.call_made_rounded,
                        value: '${r['enviados_hoy'] ?? 0}',
                        label: 'Mensajes enviados hoy',
                        color: AppColors.secondary,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  loading: () => const Padding(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 40),
                    child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
                  ),
                  error: (_, __) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 24),
                    child: Column(
                      children: [
                        const Text('No se pudo cargar el resumen.'),
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: () => ref.invalidate(resumenProvider),
                          child: const Text('Reintentar'),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                const SectionHeader(title: 'Actividad reciente'),
                ...chats.take(4).map((c) => _recentTile(context, theme, c)),
              ]),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _saludo() {
    final h = DateTime.now().hour;
    if (h < 12) return 'Buenos días,';
    if (h < 19) return 'Buenas tardes,';
    return 'Buenas noches,';
  }

  Widget _header(BuildContext context, ThemeData theme, int unread, String? initials, user) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        AppAvatar(
          initials: initials ?? 'GJ',
          radius: 24,
          colorValue: 0xFF128C7E,
          imageUrl: user?.empresa?.logo, // logo de la empresa; si no hay o no carga, iniciales
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: 12),
        Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(_saludo(),
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.6),
                  )),
              Text(user?.empresa?.nombre ?? user?.nombreCompleto ?? 'Cargando...',
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium
                      ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        IconButton.filledTonal(
          onPressed: () => context.go(Routes.notifications),
          icon: Badge(
            isLabelVisible: unread > 0,
            label: Text('$unread'),
            child: const Icon(Icons.notifications_outlined),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _recentTile(BuildContext context, ThemeData theme, chat) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: Material(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        child: InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
          onTap: () => context.push('/chats/${chat.id}'),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
              border: Border.all(color: theme.colorScheme.outline),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                AppAvatar(
                  initials: chat.contact.initials,
                  colorValue: chat.contact.avatarColorValue,
                  isOnline: chat.contact.isOnline,
                  radius: 22,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(chat.contact.name,
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700)),
                      Text(chat.lastMessage,
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(TimeFormat.relative(chat.lastTime),
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\dashboard\main_shell.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/router/app_router.dart';
import '../../core/utils/responsive.dart';

/// Scaffolds the persistent navigation (bottom bar on phones, rail on tablets).
class MainShell extends ConsumerWidget {
  const MainShell({super.key, required this.child});

  final Widget child;

  static const _destinations = [
    (Routes.dashboard, Icons.dashboard_outlined, Icons.dashboard, 'Inicio'),
    (Routes.chats, Icons.chat_bubble_outline, Icons.chat_bubble, 'Chats'),
    (
      Routes.notifications,
      Icons.notifications_outlined,
      Icons.notifications,
      'Alertas'
    ),
    (Routes.settings, Icons.settings_outlined, Icons.settings, 'Ajustes'),
  ];

  int _indexFor(BuildContext context) {
    final location = GoRouterState.of(context).uri.path;
    final i = _destinations.indexWhere((d) => location.startsWith(d.$1));
    return i < 0 ? 0 : i;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final index = _indexFor(context);
    final unread = ref.watch(unreadNotificationsProvider);
    final useRail = !Responsive.isMobile(context);

    void onTap(int i) => context.go(_destinations[i].$1);

    if (useRail) {
      return Scaffold(
        body: Row(
          children: [
            NavigationRail(
              selectedIndex: index,
              onDestinationSelected: onTap,
              labelType: NavigationRailLabelType.all,
              destinations: [
                for (final d in _destinations)
                  NavigationRailDestination(
                    icon: _withBadge(
                        Icon(d.$2), d.$1 == Routes.notifications ? unread : 0),
                    selectedIcon: _withBadge(Icon(d.$3),
                        d.$1 == Routes.notifications ? unread : 0),
                    label: Text(d.$4),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
            const VerticalDivider(width: 1),
            Expanded(child: child),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      body: child,
      bottomNavigationBar: NavigationBar(
        selectedIndex: index,
        onDestinationSelected: onTap,
        destinations: [
          for (final d in _destinations)
            NavigationDestination(
              icon: _withBadge(
                  Icon(d.$2), d.$1 == Routes.notifications ? unread : 0),
              selectedIcon: _withBadge(
                  Icon(d.$3), d.$1 == Routes.notifications ? unread : 0),
              label: d.$4,
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _withBadge(Widget icon, int count) {
    if (count <= 0) return icon;
    return Badge(label: Text('$count'), child: icon);
  }
}

```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\notifications\notifications_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../core/utils/abrir_url.dart';

import '../../core/models/app_notification.dart';
import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/services/cache_service.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../core/utils/time_format.dart';
import '../../widgets/brand_logo.dart';

class NotificationsScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const NotificationsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final items = ref.watch(notificationsProvider);
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Notificaciones'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () =>
                ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).markAllRead(),
            child: const Text('Marcar leídas'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: items.isEmpty
          ? RefreshIndicator(
              onRefresh: () => ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).load(),
              child: ListView(
                physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                children: const [
                  SizedBox(height: 120),
                  EmptyState(
                    icon: Icons.notifications_off_outlined,
                    title: 'Sin notificaciones',
                    message: 'Aquí verás tus alertas, igual que en la web.',
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            )
          : RefreshIndicator(
              onRefresh: () => ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).load(),
              child: ListView.separated(
                physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                itemCount: items.length,
                separatorBuilder: (_, __) =>
                    const Divider(height: 1, indent: 72),
                itemBuilder: (_, i) => _NotificationTile(item: items[i]),
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _NotificationTile extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _NotificationTile({required this.item});
  final AppNotification item;

  Future<void> _abrir(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    if (!item.isRead) ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).markRead(item.id);
    final ruta = item.ruta;
    if (ruta == null) return;
    final server = await CacheService().getServerUrl();
    final url = ruta.startsWith('http') ? ruta : '${server ?? ''}${ruta.startsWith('/') ? '' : '/'}$ruta';
    if (context.mounted) await abrirUrl(context, url);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final (icon, color) = switch (item.type) {
      NotificationType.message => (Icons.chat_bubble, AppColors.primary),
      NotificationType.automation => (Icons.account_tree, AppColors.n8nAccent),
      NotificationType.ai => (Icons.auto_awesome, AppColors.aiAccent),
      NotificationType.alert => (Icons.warning_amber, AppColors.warning),
      NotificationType.system => (Icons.settings, AppColors.blue),
    };

    return Container(
      color: item.isRead
          ? null
          : AppColors.primary.withValues(alpha: 0.06),
      child: ListTile(
        onTap: () => _abrir(context, ref),
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
        leading: Container(
          width: 42,
          height: 42,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          ),
          child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 22),
        ),
        title: Text(item.title,
            style: TextStyle(
                fontWeight:
                    item.isRead ? FontWeight.w600 : FontWeight.w800)),
        subtitle: Text(item.body,
            maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        trailing: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
          children: [
            Text(TimeFormat.relative(item.time),
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
            if (!item.isRead)
              Container(
                margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
                width: 8,
                height: 8,
                decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.primary,
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
              ),

class _DropdownTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DropdownTile({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.options,
    required this.onChanged,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final List<String> options;
  final ValueChanged<String> onChanged;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icon, color: theme.colorScheme.primary),
      title: Text(label),
      trailing: DropdownButton<String>(
        value: value,
        underline: const SizedBox.shrink(),
        items: options
            .map((o) => DropdownMenuItem(value: o, child: Text(o)))
            .toList(),
        onChanged: (v) {
          if (v != null) onChanged(v);
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\security\security_screen.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../../core/utils/abrir_url.dart';

import '../../core/services/biometria_service.dart';
import '../../widgets/section_card.dart';

class SecurityScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const SecurityScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<SecurityScreen> createState() => _SecurityScreenState();
}

class _SecurityScreenState extends State<SecurityScreen> {
  static const _urlPerfil = 'https://message-business.gijac.com/perfil';

  bool _biometria = false;
  bool _disponible = true;
  bool _cambiando = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cargar();
  }

  Future<void> _cargar() async {
    final activo = await BiometriaService.activo();
    final disp = await BiometriaService.disponible();
    if (mounted) {
      setState(() {
        _biometria = activo;
        _disponible = disp;
      });
    }
  }

  void _aviso(String t) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(t)));
  }

  Future<void> _cambiarBiometria(bool v) async {
    if (_cambiando) return;
    setState(() => _cambiando = true);
    final estado = await BiometriaService.cambiar(v);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _biometria = estado;
      _cambiando = false;
    });
    if (v && !estado) {
      _aviso(_disponible
          ? 'No se pudo activar el bloqueo.'
          : 'Configura una huella, rostro o bloqueo de pantalla en los ajustes del teléfono.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _abrirWeb() => abrirUrl(context, _urlPerfil, titulo: 'Mi perfil');

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Seguridad')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Autenticación'),
          GroupedCard(children: [
            AppTile(
              icon: Icons.password,
              title: 'Cambiar contraseña',
              subtitle: 'Se cambia desde la web',
              trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
              onTap: _abrirWeb,
            ),
            AppTile(
              icon: Icons.fingerprint,
              title: 'Bloqueo biométrico',
              subtitle: _disponible ? 'Huella o rostro al abrir la app' : 'No hay huella, rostro ni bloqueo de pantalla configurado',
              trailing: Switch(value: _biometria, onChanged: _cambiando ? null : _cambiarBiometria),
            ),
          ]),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\settings\settings_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../core/providers/app_providers.dart';
import '../../core/providers/user_provider.dart';
import '../../core/router/app_router.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../core/utils/abrir_url.dart';
import '../../core/services/cache_service.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import '../../widgets/section_card.dart';

class SettingsScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const SettingsScreen({super.key});

  static const _webChatbots = 'https://message-business.gijac.com/chatbots';

  Future<void> _abrirWeb(BuildContext context, String url) => abrirUrl(context, url);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final notificaciones = ref.watch(notificacionesActivasProvider);
    final notificacionesOn = notificaciones.valueOrNull ?? true;
    final userState = ref.watch(userProvider);
    final user = userState.user;

    // Calcular iniciales dinámicamente
    String initials = 'U';
    if (user != null) {
      final parts = user.nombreCompleto.split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
      initials = parts.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();
    }

    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Ajustes')),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          Material(
            color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            child: InkWell(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              onTap: () => context.push(Routes.userProfile),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  border: Border.all(color: theme.colorScheme.outline),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    AppAvatar(
                      initials: initials,             // <-- CAMBIADO (antes 'JA')
                      imageUrl: user?.foto,
                      radius: 28,
                      colorValue: 0xFF3B82F6,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            user?.nombreCompleto ?? 'Cargando...', // <-- CAMBIADO (antes 'Juan Agente')
                            style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            'Ver y editar perfil',
                            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Cuenta'),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.business_center,
                iconColor: AppColors.secondary,
                title: 'Perfil de WhatsApp Business',
                subtitle: 'Información del negocio',
                onTap: () => context.push(Routes.whatsappProfile),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Preferencias'),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.dark_mode_outlined,
                title: 'Tema',
                subtitle: switch (themeMode) {
                  ThemeMode.light => 'Claro',
                  ThemeMode.dark => 'Oscuro',
                  ThemeMode.system => 'Sistema',
                },
                trailing: SegmentedButton<ThemeMode>(
                  showSelectedIcon: false,
                  segments: const [
                    ButtonSegment(
                      value: ThemeMode.light,
                      icon: Icon(Icons.light_mode),
                    ),
                    ButtonSegment(
                      value: ThemeMode.system,
                      icon: Icon(Icons.brightness_auto),
                    ),
                    ButtonSegment(
                      value: ThemeMode.dark,
                      icon: Icon(Icons.dark_mode),
                    ),
                  ],
                  selected: {themeMode},
                  onSelectionChanged: (s) =>
                      ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier).set(s.first),
                ),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.notifications_outlined,
                title: 'Notificaciones',
                subtitle: notificacionesOn ? 'Activadas' : 'Desactivadas',
                trailing: Switch(
                  value: notificacionesOn,
                  onChanged: notificaciones.isLoading
                      ? null
                      : (v) async {
                          final ok = await ref.read(notificacionesActivasProvider.notifier).cambiar(v);
                          if (!ok && context.mounted) {
                            ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(
                              content: Text(Platform.isIOS
                                  ? 'Permiso bloqueado. Actívalo en Ajustes del iPhone → Notificaciones → GIJAC.'
                                  : 'Permiso bloqueado. Actívalo en Ajustes del teléfono → Aplicaciones → GIJAC → Notificaciones.'),
                            ));
                          }
                        },
                ),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.language,
                title: 'Idioma',
                subtitle: 'Español',
                onTap: () {},
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Automatización e IA'),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.settings_suggest_outlined,
                iconColor: AppColors.aiAccent,
                title: 'Configuración de chatbots',
                subtitle: 'Se administra en la web',
                trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
                onTap: () => _abrirWeb(context, '$_webChatbots/configuracion'),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.smart_toy_outlined,
                iconColor: AppColors.aiAccent,
                title: 'Chatbot (nodos)',
                subtitle: 'Flujos de conversación',
                trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
                onTap: () => _abrirWeb(context, '$_webChatbots/nodos'),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.auto_awesome,
                iconColor: AppColors.aiAccent,
                title: 'Asistente con IA',
                subtitle: 'Respuestas con inteligencia artificial',
                trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
                onTap: () => _abrirWeb(context, '$_webChatbots/asistente'),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.account_tree_outlined,
                iconColor: AppColors.n8nAccent,
                title: 'Integraciones n8n',
                subtitle: 'Workflows y automatizaciones',
                trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
                onTap: () => _abrirWeb(context, '$_webChatbots/n8n'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Seguridad'),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.lock_outline,
                title: 'Seguridad',
                onTap: () => context.push(Routes.security),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          const SectionHeader(title: 'Soporte'),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.help_outline,
                title: 'Centro de ayuda y soporte',
                onTap: () => context.push(Routes.support),
              ),
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.info_outline,
                title: 'Acerca de',
                subtitle: 'Versión 1.0.0',
                onTap: () {},
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.logout,
                iconColor: AppColors.danger,
                title: 'Cerrar sesión',
                onTap: () async {
                  final cache = CacheService();
                  await cache.clearDeviceData();
                  await cache.clearProfile();

                  if (context.mounted) {
                    appRouter.go(Routes.qrLogin);
                  }
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 32),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class SupportScreen extends StatelessWidget {

  @override

              ),

                question: '¿Por qué no me llegan las notificaciones?',

En algunos teléfonos (Oppo, Xiaomi, etc.) cerrar la app deslizándola desde recientes detiene los avisos; bloquéala en recientes para evitarlo.',

              const _FaqTile(
                question: '¿Dónde administro el catálogo?',
                answer: 'Desde la web. En Ajustes > Perfil de WhatsApp Business > Catálogo se abre directamente.',

              _enlace(context, Icons.description_outlined, 'Términos y condiciones', null, _urlTerminos),
              _enlace(context, Icons.privacy_tip_outlined, 'Políticas de privacidad', null, _urlPrivacidad),

            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          Center(
            child: Text('GIJAC v1.0.0', style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _enlace(BuildContext context, IconData icono, String titulo, String? subtitulo, String url, {bool abrirFuera = true}) {
    final color = Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary;
    return ListTile(
      leading: Icon(icono, color: color),
      title: Text(titulo),
      subtitle: subtitulo == null ? null : Text(subtitulo),
      trailing: Icon(abrirFuera ? Icons.open_in_new : Icons.chevron_right, size: 18),
      onTap: () => abrirUrl(context, url, titulo: titulo),
    );
  }
}

class _FaqTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _FaqTile({required this.question, required this.answer});

  final String question;
  final String answer;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return ExpansionTile(
      tilePadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
      childrenPadding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 16),
      leading: Icon(Icons.help_outline, color: theme.colorScheme.primary),
      title: Text(question, style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
      children: [
        Align(
          alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
          child: Text(answer, style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant, height: 1.5)),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```
