### ARCHIVO: lib\features\user_profile\edit_profile_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';

import '../../core/constants/paises_telefono.dart';
import '../../core/providers/user_provider.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';

/// Editar nombre, apellido, teléfono y la foto de perfil (la misma que se ve en la web).
class EditProfileScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const EditProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<EditProfileScreen> createState() => _EditProfileScreenState();
}

class _EditProfileScreenState extends ConsumerState<EditProfileScreen> {
  late final TextEditingController _nombre;
  late final TextEditingController _apellido;
  late final TextEditingController _telefono;
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  bool _guardando = false;
  bool _subiendoFoto = false;
  final _identificacion = TextEditingController();
  Map<String, List<Map<String, String>>>? _opciones;
  List<Map<String, String>> _ciudades = [];
  String? _generoId, _tipoDocId, _paisId, _ciudadId;
  bool _cargandoCiudades = false;
  String? _errorOpciones;
  late PaisTelefono _pais;
  late final bool _indicativoConMas; // se respeta el formato con que ya está guardado ("57" o "+57")

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final u = ref.read(userProvider).user;
    _nombre = TextEditingController(text: u?.nombre ?? '');
    _apellido = TextEditingController(text: u?.apellido ?? '');
    _telefono = TextEditingController(text: u?.telefono ?? '');
    _pais = PaisTelefono.porIndicativo(u?.codigoTelefono) ?? PaisTelefono.colombia;
    _indicativoConMas = (u?.codigoTelefono ?? '').startsWith('+');
    _identificacion.text = u?.identificacion ?? '';
    _generoId = u?.generoId;
    _tipoDocId = u?.tipoIdentificacion;
    _paisId = u?.paisId;
    _ciudadId = u?.codCiudad;
    _cargarOpciones();
  }

  Future<void> _cargarOpciones() async {
    try {
      final o = await ApiService().getOpcionesPerfil();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _opciones = o;
        _errorOpciones = null;
        // Un valor guardado que ya no existe en la lista se descarta para no romper el desplegable
        if (!o['generos']!.any((e) => e['id'] == _generoId)) _generoId = null;
        if (!o['documentos']!.any((e) => e['id'] == _tipoDocId)) _tipoDocId = null;
        if (!o['paises']!.any((e) => e['id'] == _paisId)) _paisId = null;
      });
      if (_paisId != null) await _cargarCiudades(_paisId!, conservar: true);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _errorOpciones = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _errorOpciones = 'No se pudieron cargar las opciones.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cargarCiudades(String paisId, {bool conservar = false}) async {
    setState(() {
      _cargandoCiudades = true;
      if (!conservar) _ciudadId = null;
    });
    try {
      final c = await ApiService().getCiudades(paisId);
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _ciudades = c;
        if (!c.any((e) => e['id'] == _ciudadId)) _ciudadId = null;
      });
    } catch (_) {
      _aviso('No se pudieron cargar las ciudades.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _cargandoCiudades = false);
    }
  }

  /// Hoja con buscador para países y ciudades (las listas pueden ser largas)
  Future<String?> _elegirDeLista(String titulo, List<Map<String, String>> items, String? actual) {
    return showModalBottomSheet<String>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (_) => _SelectorLista(titulo: titulo, items: items, actual: actual),
    );
  }

  String _nombreDe(List<Map<String, String>>? items, String? id) {
    if (items == null || id == null) return '';
    for (final e in items) {
      if (e['id'] == id) return e['nombre']!;
    }
    return '';
  }

  Widget _campoSelector({required String label, required String valor, required VoidCallback? onTap, bool cargando = false}) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: InputDecorator(
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          labelText: label,
          border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
          suffixIcon: cargando
              ? const Padding(padding: EdgeInsets.all(14), child: SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2)))
              : const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down),
        ),
        child: Text(valor.isEmpty ? ' ' : valor, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nombre.dispose();
    _apellido.dispose();
    _telefono.dispose();
    _identificacion.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _aviso(String texto) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(texto)));
  }

  Future<void> _guardar() async {
    if (_opciones == null) {
      _aviso('Espera a que carguen las opciones del formulario.');
      return;
    }
    final formOk = _formKey.currentState!.validate();
    if (_paisId == null || _ciudadId == null) {
      _aviso('Selecciona el país y la ciudad.');
      return;
    }
    if (!formOk || _guardando) return;
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() => _guardando = true);
    try {
      final perfil = await ApiService().updateProfile({
        'nombre': _nombre.text.trim(),
        'apellido': _apellido.text.trim(),
        'genero': _generoId,
        'tipo_identificacion': _tipoDocId,
        'identificacion': _identificacion.text.trim(),
        'telefono': _telefono.text.trim(),
        'codigo_telefono': '${_indicativoConMas ? '+' : ''}${_pais.dial}',
        'pais_id': _paisId,
        'cod_ciudad': _ciudadId,
      });
      await ref.read(userProvider.notifier).setUser(perfil);
      _aviso('Perfil actualizado');
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      _aviso(e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      _aviso('No se pudo guardar. Intenta de nuevo.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _guardando = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _elegirPais() async {
    final elegido = await showModalBottomSheet<PaisTelefono>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (_) => _SelectorPais(actual: _pais),
    );
    if (elegido != null) setState(() => _pais = elegido);
  }

  Future<void> _cambiarFoto() async {
    final fuente = await showModalBottomSheet<ImageSource>(
      context: context,
      builder: (c) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_camera_outlined),
              title: const Text('Tomar foto'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(c, ImageSource.camera),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined),
              title: const Text('Elegir de la galería'),
              onTap: () => Navigator.pop(c, ImageSource.gallery),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (fuente == null) return;

    try {
      // Se reduce antes de subir: el servidor acepta hasta 2 MB
      final x = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: fuente, imageQuality: 80, maxWidth: 1024, maxHeight: 1024);
      if (x == null) return;
      setState(() => _subiendoFoto = true);
      final perfil = await ApiService().uploadProfilePhoto(File(x.path));
      await ref.read(userProvider.notifier).setUser(perfil);
      _aviso('Foto actualizada');
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      _aviso(e.message);
    } catch (e) {
      _aviso('No se pudo cambiar la foto.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _subiendoFoto = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final user = ref.watch(userProvider).user;
    final partes = (user?.nombreCompleto ?? '').split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
    final iniciales = partes.isEmpty ? 'U' : partes.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Editar perfil'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: _guardando ? null : _guardar,
            child: _guardando
                ? const SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                : const Text('Guardar'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Form(
          key: _formKey,
          child: ListView(
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 16 + MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
            keyboardDismissBehavior: ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
            children: [
              Center(
                child: GestureDetector(
                  onTap: _subiendoFoto ? null : _cambiarFoto,
                  child: Stack(
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    children: [
                      AppAvatar(initials: iniciales, radius: 56, colorValue: 0xFF3B82F6, imageUrl: user?.foto),
                      if (_subiendoFoto)
                        Positioned.fill(
                          child: DecoratedBox(
                            decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black38, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                            child: const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white)),
                          ),
                        ),
                      Positioned(
                        right: 0,
                        bottom: 0,
                        child: CircleAvatar(
                          radius: 18,
                          backgroundColor: theme.colorScheme.primary,
                          child: const Icon(Icons.photo_camera, size: 18, color: Colors.white),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Center(
                child: TextButton(
                  onPressed: _subiendoFoto ? null : _cambiarFoto,
                  child: const Text('Cambiar foto'),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _nombre,
                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                maxLength: 100,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Nombre', border: OutlineInputBorder(), counterText: ''),
                validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) ? 'Escribe tu nombre' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _apellido,
                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                maxLength: 100,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Apellido', border: OutlineInputBorder(), counterText: ''),
                validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) ? 'Escribe tu apellido' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              if (_errorOpciones != null)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
                  child: Row(children: [
                    Expanded(child: Text(_errorOpciones!, style: TextStyle(color: theme.colorScheme.error))),
                    TextButton(onPressed: _cargarOpciones, child: const Text('Reintentar')),
                  ]),
                ),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                value: _generoId,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Género', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                items: (_opciones?['generos'] ?? []).map((e) => DropdownMenuItem(value: e['id'], child: Text(e['nombre']!))).toList(),
                onChanged: _opciones == null ? null : (v) => setState(() => _generoId = v),
                validator: (v) => v == null ? 'Selecciona el género' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                value: _tipoDocId,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Tipo de identificación', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                items: (_opciones?['documentos'] ?? []).map((e) => DropdownMenuItem(value: e['id'], child: Text(e['nombre']!))).toList(),
                onChanged: _opciones == null ? null : (v) => setState(() => _tipoDocId = v),
                validator: (v) => v == null ? 'Selecciona el tipo de identificación' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _identificacion,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                inputFormatters: [FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly],
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Número de identificación', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) ? 'Escribe tu número de identificación' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  InkWell(
                    onTap: _elegirPais,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                    child: Container(
                      height: 56,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        border: Border.all(color: theme.colorScheme.outline),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text(_pais.bandera, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 22)),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          Text('+${_pais.dial}'),
                          const Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: TextFormField(
                      controller: _telefono,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                      inputFormatters: [FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly, LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(15)],
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Teléfono', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                      validator: (v) {
                        final t = v?.trim() ?? '';
                        if (t.isEmpty) return 'Escribe tu teléfono';
                        if (t.length < 6) return 'Número demasiado corto';
                        return null;
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              _campoSelector(
                label: 'País',
                valor: _nombreDe(_opciones?['paises'], _paisId),
                onTap: _opciones == null
                    ? null
                    : () async {
                        final id = await _elegirDeLista('País', _opciones!['paises']!, _paisId);
                        if (id != null && id != _paisId) {
                          setState(() => _paisId = id);
                          await _cargarCiudades(id);
                        }
                      },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              _campoSelector(
                label: 'Ciudad',
                valor: _nombreDe(_ciudades, _ciudadId),
                cargando: _cargandoCiudades,
                onTap: (_paisId == null || _cargandoCiudades)
                    ? null
                    : () async {
                        final id = await _elegirDeLista('Ciudad', _ciudades, _ciudadId);
                        if (id != null) setState(() => _ciudadId = id);
                      },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                initialValue: user?.email ?? '',
                readOnly: true,
                enabled: false,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Correo',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                  helperText: 'El correo y la contraseña se cambian desde la web.',
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Hoja inferior con buscador para elegir el país del indicativo
class _SelectorPais extends StatefulWidget {
  const _SelectorPais({required this.actual});

  final PaisTelefono actual;

  @override
  State<_SelectorPais> createState() => _SelectorPaisState();
}

class _SelectorPaisState extends State<_SelectorPais> {
  String _q = '';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final q = _q.trim().toLowerCase().replaceAll('+', '');
    final paises = q.isEmpty
        ? PaisTelefono.lista
        : PaisTelefono.lista.where((p) => p.nombre.toLowerCase().contains(q) || p.dial.startsWith(q) || p.iso.toLowerCase() == q).toList();

    return SizedBox(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.7,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 8),
              child: TextField(
                autofocus: false,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search),
                  hintText: 'Buscar país o indicativo',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                  isDense: true,
                ),
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _q = v),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: paises.isEmpty
                  ? const Center(child: Text('Sin resultados'))
                  : ListView.builder(
                      itemCount: paises.length,
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) {
                        final p = paises[i];
                        final seleccionado = p.iso == widget.actual.iso;
                        return ListTile(
                          leading: Text(p.bandera, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24)),
                          title: Text(p.nombre),
                          trailing: Text('+${p.dial}', style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium),
                          selected: seleccionado,
                          onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, p),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Hoja inferior con buscador para elegir de una lista (país, ciudad)
class _SelectorLista extends StatefulWidget {
  const _SelectorLista({required this.titulo, required this.items, required this.actual});

  final String titulo;
  final List<Map<String, String>> items;
  final String? actual;

  @override
  State<_SelectorLista> createState() => _SelectorListaState();
}

class _SelectorListaState extends State<_SelectorLista> {
  String _q = '';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final q = _q.trim().toLowerCase();
    final lista = q.isEmpty ? widget.items : widget.items.where((e) => e['nombre']!.toLowerCase().contains(q)).toList();
    return SizedBox(
      height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.7,
      child: Padding(
        padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, 8),
              child: TextField(
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search),
                  hintText: 'Buscar ${widget.titulo.toLowerCase()}',
                  border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
                  isDense: true,
                ),
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _q = v),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: lista.isEmpty
                  ? const Center(child: Text('Sin resultados'))
                  : ListView.builder(
                      itemCount: lista.length,
                      itemBuilder: (_, i) => ListTile(
                        title: Text(lista[i]['nombre']!),
                        selected: lista[i]['id'] == widget.actual,
                        onTap: () => Navigator.pop(context, lista[i]['id']),
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class UserProfileScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const UserProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final userState = ref.watch(userProvider);
    final user = userState.user!;

    // Calcular iniciales dinámicamente
    final parts = user.nombreCompleto.split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
    final initials = parts.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();
    
    // Obtener el primer rol o un valor por defecto
    final role = user.roles.isNotEmpty ? user.roles.first.capitalize() : 'Sin rol asignado';

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Perfil de usuario'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: () async {
              final ok = await ref.read(userProvider.notifier).refreshProfile();
              if (context.mounted) {
                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                  ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
                  ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(ok ? 'Perfil actualizado' : 'No se pudo actualizar. Revisa tu conexión.')));
              }
            },
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).push(
              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const EditProfileScreen()),
            ),
            child: const Text('Editar'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        children: [
          Center(
            child: Column(
              children: [
                AppAvatar(
                  initials: initials, // Antes: user.initials
                  imageUrl: user.foto,
                  radius: 48,
                  colorValue: 0xFF3B82F6, // Antes: user.avatarColorValue
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 12),

                Text(
                  user.nombreCompleto, // Antes: user.name
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 4),

                Text(
                  role, // Antes: user.role
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(
                      alpha: 0.6,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(height: 8),

                StatusBadge(
                  label: user.verificado ? 'Verificado' : 'No verificado', // Adaptado al dato real
                  color: user.verificado ? AppColors.primary : Colors.grey,
                  icon: Icons.verified,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: _miniStat(
                  theme,
                  '1.2k',
                  'Atendidos',
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: _miniStat(
                  theme,
                  '98%',
                  'Satisfacción',
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: _miniStat(
                  theme,
                  '2.1m',
                  'Resp. media',
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 24),

          const SectionHeader(
            title: 'Datos personales',
          ),

          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.person_outline,
                title: user.nombreCompleto, // Antes: user.name
                subtitle: 'Nombre completo',
              ),

              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.alternate_email,
                title: user.email,
                subtitle: 'Correo corporativo',
              ),

              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.phone_outlined,
                title: user.numeroCompleto ?? 'No registrado', // Antes: user.phone
                subtitle: 'Teléfono',
              ),

              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
                title: user.id, // Antes: user.employeeCode
                subtitle: 'ID de usuario',
              ),
            ],
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          const SectionHeader(
            title: 'Rol y permisos',
          ),

          GroupedCard(
            children: [
              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
                iconColor: AppColors.secondary,
                title: role, // Antes: user.role
                subtitle: 'Rol asignado',
              ),

              AppTile(
                icon: Icons.business_outlined, // Cambiado a ícono de empresa
                title: user.empresa?.nombre ?? 'Sin empresa', // Antes: user.department
                subtitle: 'Empresa',
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _miniStat(
    ThemeData theme,
    String value,
    String label,
  ) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        vertical: 16,
      ),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(
          color: theme.colorScheme.outline,
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(
            value,
            style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
              color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface.withValues(
                alpha: 0.6,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// Extensión auxiliar para capitalizar la primera letra
extension StringCapitalization on String {
  String capitalize() {
    if (isEmpty) return this;
    return '${this[0].toUpperCase()}${substring(1)}';
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\whatsapp_profile\edit_whatsapp_profile_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';

import '../../core/models/whatsapp_business_profile.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';

/// Editar el perfil de WhatsApp Business (solo números de producción). Mismos campos que la web.
class EditWhatsappProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const EditWhatsappProfileScreen({super.key, required this.perfil});

  final WhatsappBusinessProfile perfil;

  @override
  State<EditWhatsappProfileScreen> createState() => _EditWhatsappProfileScreenState();
}

class _EditWhatsappProfileScreenState extends State<EditWhatsappProfileScreen> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  late final TextEditingController _about;
  late final TextEditingController _email;
  late final TextEditingController _descripcion;
  late final TextEditingController _direccion;
  late final TextEditingController _web1;
  late final TextEditingController _web2;
  String? _vertical;
  bool _guardando = false;
  bool _subiendoFoto = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final p = widget.perfil;
    _about = TextEditingController(text: p.about);
    _email = TextEditingController(text: p.email);
    _descripcion = TextEditingController(text: p.description);
    _direccion = TextEditingController(text: p.address);
    _web1 = TextEditingController(text: p.websites.isNotEmpty ? p.websites[0] : '');
    _web2 = TextEditingController(text: p.websites.length > 1 ? p.websites[1] : '');
    _vertical = p.categorias.any((c) => c['id'] == p.vertical) ? p.vertical : null;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final c in [_about, _email, _descripcion, _direccion, _web1, _web2]) {
      c.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _aviso(String t) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
      ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(t)));
  }

  String? _validarWeb(String? v) {
    final t = v?.trim() ?? '';
    if (t.isEmpty) return null;
    final u = Uri.tryParse(t);
    return (u != null && (u.scheme == 'http' || u.scheme == 'https') && u.host.isNotEmpty) ? null : 'Debe empezar por https://';
  }

  Future<void> _guardar() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate() || _guardando) return;
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() => _guardando = true);
    try {
      final nuevo = await ApiService().updateWhatsappProfile({
        'about': _about.text.trim(),
        'email': _email.text.trim(),
        'vertical': _vertical,
        'description': _descripcion.text.trim(),
        'address': _direccion.text.trim(),
        'websites': [_web1.text.trim(), _web2.text.trim()].where((e) => e.isNotEmpty).toList(),
      });
      _aviso('Perfil de WhatsApp actualizado');
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop(nuevo);
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      _aviso(e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      _aviso('No se pudo guardar. Intenta de nuevo.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _guardando = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _cambiarFoto() async {
    final fuente = await showModalBottomSheet<ImageSource>(
      context: context,
      builder: (c) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(children: [
          ListTile(leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_camera_outlined), title: const Text('Tomar foto'), onTap: () => Navigator.pop(c, ImageSource.camera)),
          ListTile(leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined), title: const Text('Elegir de la galería'), onTap: () => Navigator.pop(c, ImageSource.gallery)),
        ]),
      ),
    );
    if (fuente == null) return;
    try {
      final x = await ImagePicker().pickImage(source: fuente, imageQuality: 85, maxWidth: 1024, maxHeight: 1024);
      if (x == null) return;
      setState(() => _subiendoFoto = true);
      _aviso(await ApiService().uploadWhatsappPhoto(File(x.path)));
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      _aviso(e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      _aviso('No se pudo subir la foto.');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _subiendoFoto = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final p = widget.perfil;
    final partes = p.nombre.split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
    final iniciales = partes.isEmpty ? 'WB' : partes.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Editar perfil de WhatsApp'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: _guardando ? null : _guardar,
            child: _guardando
                ? const SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                : const Text('Guardar'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Form(
          key: _formKey,
          child: ListView(
            padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 16 + MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom),
            keyboardDismissBehavior: ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
            children: [
              Center(
                child: GestureDetector(
                  onTap: _subiendoFoto ? null : _cambiarFoto,
                  child: Stack(
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    children: [
                      AppAvatar(initials: iniciales, radius: 56, colorValue: 0xFF128C7E, imageUrl: p.foto),
                      if (_subiendoFoto)
                        const Positioned.fill(
                          child: DecoratedBox(
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black38, shape: BoxShape.circle),
                            child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: Colors.white)),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Center(child: TextButton(onPressed: _subiendoFoto ? null : _cambiarFoto, child: const Text('Cambiar foto'))),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _about,
                maxLength: 139,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Info', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: (v) => (v == null || v.trim().isEmpty) ? 'La info es requerida' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _email,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Email', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: (v) {
                  final t = v?.trim() ?? '';
                  if (t.isEmpty) return 'El email es requerido';
                  return RegExp(r'^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$').hasMatch(t) ? null : 'Email no válido';
                },
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                value: _vertical,
                isExpanded: true,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Categoría', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                items: p.categorias.map((c) => DropdownMenuItem(value: c['id'], child: Text(c['nombre']!))).toList(),
                onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _vertical = v),
                validator: (v) => v == null ? 'Selecciona la categoría' : null,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _descripcion,
                maxLength: 512,
                maxLines: 4,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Descripción', border: OutlineInputBorder(), alignLabelWithHint: true),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _direccion,
                maxLength: 256,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Dirección', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _web1,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.url,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Sitio web 1', hintText: 'https://www.ejemplo.com', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: _validarWeb,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              TextFormField(
                controller: _web2,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.url,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Sitio web 2 (opcional)', hintText: 'https://www.ejemplo.com', border: OutlineInputBorder()),
                validator: _validarWeb,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\features\whatsapp_profile\whatsapp_profile_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../core/models/whatsapp_business_profile.dart';
import '../../core/services/api_service.dart';
import '../../core/theme/app_colors.dart';
import '../../core/utils/abrir_url.dart';
import '../../widgets/app_avatar.dart';
import '../../widgets/section_card.dart';
import '../../widgets/status_badge.dart';
import 'edit_whatsapp_profile_screen.dart';

/// Perfil de WhatsApp Business del número activo (datos reales). Editable solo en números de producción.
class WhatsappProfileScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const WhatsappProfileScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<WhatsappProfileScreen> createState() => _WhatsappProfileScreenState();
}

class _WhatsappProfileScreenState extends State<WhatsappProfileScreen> {
  static const _urlCatalogo = 'https://message-business.gijac.com/catalogo';

  WhatsappBusinessProfile? _perfil;
  String? _error;
  bool _cargando = true;
  bool _sinConexion = false; // se muestra lo guardado en el teléfono
  static const _kCache = 'wa_business_profile_cache';

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _cargar();
  }

  Future<void> _leerCache() async {
    try {
      final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final txt = sp.getString(_kCache);
      if (txt != null && _perfil == null) {
        final p = WhatsappBusinessProfile.fromJson(jsonDecode(txt) as Map<String, dynamic>);
        if (mounted) setState(() => _perfil = p);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _guardarCache(WhatsappBusinessProfile p) async {
    try {
      final sp = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await sp.setString(_kCache, jsonEncode(p.toJson()));
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// Muestra al instante lo último guardado y lo pone al día con el servidor; sin internet se queda lo guardado
  Future<void> _cargar() async {
    setState(() {
      _cargando = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    await _leerCache();
    try {
      final p = await ApiService().getWhatsappProfile();
      await _guardarCache(p);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _perfil = p;
          _sinConexion = false;
        });
      }
    } on ApiException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = e.message;
          _sinConexion = _perfil != null;
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _error = 'No se pudo cargar el perfil.';
          _sinConexion = _perfil != null;
        });
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _cargando = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _editar() async {
    final p = _perfil;
    if (p == null) return;
    final nuevo = await Navigator.of(context).push<WhatsappBusinessProfile>(
      MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => EditWhatsappProfileScreen(perfil: p)),
    );
    if (nuevo != null && mounted) {
      setState(() => _perfil = nuevo);
      await _guardarCache(nuevo);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _abrir(String url) => abrirUrl(context, url);

  String _iniciales(String nombre) {
    final partes = nombre.split(' ').where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).take(2).toList();
    return partes.isEmpty ? 'WB' : partes.map((s) => s[0].toUpperCase()).join();
  }

  String _o(String v, String vacio) => v.trim().isEmpty ? vacio : v;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final p = _perfil;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Perfil de WhatsApp Business'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(tooltip: 'Actualizar', icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh), onPressed: _cargando ? null : _cargar),
          if (p != null && p.editable) TextButton(onPressed: _editar, child: const Text('Editar')),
        ],
      ),
      body: _cargando && p == null
          ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
          : p == null
              ? Center(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Text(_error ?? 'No se pudo cargar el perfil.', textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        FilledButton(onPressed: _cargar, child: const Text('Reintentar')),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                )
              : RefreshIndicator(
                  onRefresh: _cargar,
                  child: ListView(
                    physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                    children: [
                      Center(
                        child: Column(
                          children: [
                            AppAvatar(initials: _iniciales(p.nombre), radius: 48, colorValue: 0xFF128C7E, imageUrl: p.foto),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(_o(p.nombre, 'Sin nombre'),
                                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            p.esPrueba
                                ? const StatusBadge(label: 'Número de prueba', color: Colors.orange, icon: Icons.science_outlined)
                                : const StatusBadge(label: 'Número de producción', color: AppColors.primary, icon: Icons.verified),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (_sinConexion)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                          child: Text('Sin conexión: mostrando la última información guardada.',
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall),
                        ),
                      if (p.avisoMeta != null)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                          child: Text('Meta no devolvió los datos del perfil: ${p.avisoMeta}',
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(color: theme.colorScheme.error)),
                        ),
                      if (p.esPrueba)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 16),
                          child: Text(
                            'Meta no permite editar el perfil de los números de prueba. Solo se puede consultar.',
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
                          ),
                        ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 24),
                      const SectionHeader(title: 'Información del negocio'),
                      GroupedCard(children: [
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.info_outline, title: _o(p.about, 'Sin información'), subtitle: 'Info'),
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.category_outlined, title: _o(p.categoriaNombre, 'Sin categoría'), subtitle: 'Categoría'),
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.description_outlined, title: _o(p.description, 'Sin descripción'), subtitle: 'Descripción'),
                      ]),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      const SectionHeader(title: 'Contacto'),
                      GroupedCard(children: [
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.phone, title: _o(p.telefono, 'Sin teléfono'), subtitle: 'Teléfono comercial'),
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.email_outlined, title: _o(p.email, 'Sin correo'), subtitle: 'Correo'),
                        if (p.websites.isEmpty)
                          const AppTile(icon: Icons.language, title: 'Sin sitio web', subtitle: 'Sitio web')
                        else
                          for (final w in p.websites) AppTile(icon: Icons.language, title: w, subtitle: 'Sitio web', onTap: () => _abrir(w)),
                        AppTile(icon: Icons.location_on_outlined, title: _o(p.address, 'Sin dirección'), subtitle: 'Dirección'),
                      ]),
                      const SizedBox(height: 20),
                      // TODO: Horario de atención (oculto por ahora)
                      const SectionHeader(title: 'Catálogo'),
                      GroupedCard(children: [
                        AppTile(
                          icon: Icons.storefront_outlined,
                          title: 'Catálogo',
                          subtitle: 'Gestiónalo desde la web',
                          trailing: const Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 18),
                          onTap: () => _abrir(_urlCatalogo),

                      ]),
                    ],
                  ),
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\widgets\app_avatar.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Circular avatar with colored initials and optional online dot.
class AppAvatar extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppAvatar({
    super.key,
    required this.initials,
    this.colorValue = 0xFF25D366,
    this.radius = 24,
    this.isOnline = false,
    this.imageUrl,
  });

  final String initials;
  final int colorValue;
  final double radius;
  final bool isOnline;

  /// Foto de perfil (URL). Si falta o no carga, se muestran las iniciales.
  final String? imageUrl;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final color = Color(colorValue);
    final iniciales = Text(
      initials,
      style: TextStyle(
        color: color,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
        fontSize: radius * 0.7,
      ),
    );
    return Stack(
      children: [
        CircleAvatar(
          radius: radius,
          backgroundColor: color.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
          child: (imageUrl == null || imageUrl!.isEmpty)
              ? iniciales
              : ClipOval(
                  child: Image.network(
                    imageUrl!,
                    width: radius * 2,
                    height: radius * 2,
                    fit: BoxFit.cover,
                    cacheWidth: (radius * 2 * MediaQuery.devicePixelRatioOf(context)).round(),
                    errorBuilder: (_, __, ___) => iniciales,
                  ),
                ),
        ),
        if (isOnline)
          Positioned(
            right: 0,
            bottom: 0,
            child: Container(
              width: radius * 0.45,
              height: radius * 0.45,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFF22C55E),
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
                  width: 2,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\widgets\app_gate.dart
```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../core/services/biometria_service.dart';

/// Envuelve toda la app:
///  - deja espacio para la barra de navegación/gestos del teléfono (que no tape el contenido)
///  - aplica el bloqueo biométrico al abrir y al volver tras un rato en segundo plano
class AppGate extends StatefulWidget {
  const AppGate({super.key, required this.child});

  final Widget child;

  @override
  State<AppGate> createState() => _AppGateState();
}

class _AppGateState extends State<AppGate> with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  static const _gracia = Duration(seconds: 30); // cámara/galería/permisos no deben bloquear al volver
  bool _bloqueado = false;
  bool _verificando = false;
  DateTime? _salida;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _bloquearSiActivo(); // al abrir la app
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _bloquearSiActivo() async {
    if (await BiometriaService.activo() && mounted) {
      setState(() => _bloqueado = true);
      _pedir();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _pedir() async {
    if (_verificando) return;
    _verificando = true;
    final ok = await BiometriaService.autenticar('Desbloquea GIJAC');
    _verificando = false;
    if (ok && mounted) setState(() => _bloqueado = false);
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (_verificando) return; // el propio diálogo del sistema pausa la app
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.paused || state == AppLifecycleState.hidden) {
      _salida ??= DateTime.now();
    } else if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
      final salida = _salida;
      _salida = null;
      if (salida != null && DateTime.now().difference(salida) > _gracia) _bloquearSiActivo();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final fondo = Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor;
    // SafeArea abajo: la barra del sistema (Android 15 dibuja la app detrás) y el indicador de inicio de iPhone.
    // Arriba no: cada pantalla ya maneja la barra de estado con su AppBar.
    final contenido = ColoredBox(
      color: fondo,
      child: SafeArea(top: false, child: widget.child),
    );
    if (!_bloqueado) return contenido;

    final tema = Theme.of(context);
    return Stack(
      children: [
        contenido,
        Positioned.fill(
          child: Material(
            color: fondo,
            child: SafeArea(
              child: Center(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 56, color: tema.colorScheme.primary),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    Text('GIJAC bloqueada', style: tema.textTheme.titleLarge),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    FilledButton.icon(
                      onPressed: _pedir,
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.fingerprint),
                      label: const Text('Desbloquear'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Section title used above lists / grids.
class SectionHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const SectionHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    this.action,
    this.onAction,
  });

  final String title;
  final String? action;
  final VoidCallback? onAction;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: Theme.of(context)
                .textTheme
                .titleMedium
                ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
          ),
          if (action != null)
            TextButton(
              onPressed: onAction,
              child: Text(action!),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A rounded card grouping ListTiles (used in settings/profile).
class GroupedCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const GroupedCard({super.key, required this.children});

  final List<Widget> children;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    // Material (y no un Container con color) para que los ListTile pinten su fondo y su efecto al tocar
    return Material(
      color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        side: BorderSide(color: theme.colorScheme.outline),
      ),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          for (int i = 0; i < children.length; i++) ...[
            children[i],
            if (i != children.length - 1)
              Divider(
                height: 1,
                indent: 56,
                color: theme.colorScheme.outline,
              ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A titled section: a [SectionHeader] above a [GroupedCard].
class SectionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const SectionCard({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.children,
  });

  final String title;
  final List<Widget> children;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 8),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          SectionHeader(title: title),
          GroupedCard(children: children),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A consistent settings/info row.
class AppTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const AppTile({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    this.subtitle,
    this.iconColor,
    this.trailing,
    this.onTap,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  final Color? iconColor;
  final Widget? trailing;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final color = iconColor ?? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primary;
    return ListTile(
      onTap: onTap,
      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
      leading: Container(
        width: 38,
        height: 38,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
      ),
      title: Text(title),
      subtitle: subtitle != null ? Text(subtitle!) : null,
      trailing: trailing ??
          (onTap != null
              ? const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 22)
              : null),
    );
  }
}
```

```python
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Statistic card with icon, value, label and optional trend.
class StatCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const StatCard({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.value,
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
    this.trend,
    this.trendUp = true,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String value;
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final String? trend;
  final bool trendUp;

    @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12), // Reducido de 16 a 12
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: theme.colorScheme.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: theme.colorScheme.outline),
      ),
      child: Column(

        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,

                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8), // Reducido de 10 a 8

                  color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                ),
                child: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20), // Reducido de 22 a 20
              ),
              if (trend != null)

                      trendUp ? Icons.trending_up : Icons.trending_down,
```

### ARCHIVO: lib\widgets\web_view_screen.dart
```python
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';
import 'package:webview_flutter/webview_flutter.dart';
import 'package:webview_flutter_android/webview_flutter_android.dart';

/// Páginas externas (web de GIJAC, políticas, etc.) dentro de la app.
class WebViewScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const WebViewScreen({super.key, required this.url, this.titulo});

  final String url;
  final String? titulo;

  @override
  State<WebViewScreen> createState() => _WebViewScreenState();
}

class _WebViewScreenState extends State<WebViewScreen> {
  late final WebViewController _controller;
  int _progreso = 0;
  bool _error = false;
  bool _puedeVolver = false;
  String? _titulo;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _titulo = widget.titulo;

    _controller = WebViewController()
      ..setJavaScriptMode(JavaScriptMode.unrestricted)
      ..setNavigationDelegate(
        NavigationDelegate(
          onProgress: (p) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _progreso = p);
          },
          onPageStarted: (_) {
            if (mounted) setState(() => _error = false);
          },
          onPageFinished: (_) async {
            final volver = await _controller.canGoBack();
            final t = await _controller.getTitle();
            if (!mounted) return;
            setState(() {
              _puedeVolver = volver;
              if (widget.titulo == null && t != null && t.isNotEmpty) _titulo = t;
            });
          },
          onWebResourceError: (e) {
            // Solo se muestra el error si falla la página principal (no imágenes ni scripts sueltos)
            if (e.isForMainFrame ?? true) {
              if (mounted) setState(() => _error = true);
            }
          },
          onNavigationRequest: (req) {
            final uri = Uri.tryParse(req.url);
            // mailto:, tel:, whatsapp:, intent:… se abren con la app del teléfono
            if (uri != null && uri.scheme != 'http' && uri.scheme != 'https' && uri.scheme != 'about') {
              launchUrl(uri, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication).catchError((_) => false);
              return NavigationDecision.prevent;
            }
            return NavigationDecision.navigate;
          },
        ),
      );

    // Android: permitir que los formularios de la web suban archivos (<input type="file">)
    final plataforma = _controller.platform;
    if (plataforma is AndroidWebViewController) {
      plataforma.setOnShowFileSelector(_elegirArchivos);
    }

    _controller.loadRequest(Uri.parse(widget.url));
  }

  Future<List<String>> _elegirArchivos(FileSelectorParams params) async {
    try {
      final r = await FilePicker.platform.pickFiles(
        allowMultiple: params.mode == FileSelectorMode.openMultiple,
      );
      if (r == null) return [];
      return r.paths.whereType<String>().map((p) => File(p).uri.toString()).toList();
    } catch (_) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return PopScope(
      canPop: !_puedeVolver,
      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, _) async {
        if (!didPop && _puedeVolver) await _controller.goBack();
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          leading: IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.close),
            tooltip: 'Cerrar',
            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
          ),
          title: Text(_titulo ?? 'GIJAC', overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
              tooltip: 'Recargar',
              onPressed: () => _controller.reload(),
            ),
          ],
          bottom: _progreso < 100 && !_error
              ? PreferredSize(
                  preferredSize: const Size.fromHeight(3),
                  child: LinearProgressIndicator(value: _progreso / 100, minHeight: 3),
                )
              : null,
        ),
        body: _error
            ? Center(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      const Icon(Icons.wifi_off, size: 48),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      const Text('No se pudo cargar la página. Revisa tu conexión.', textAlign: TextAlign.center),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      FilledButton(
                        onPressed: () {
                          setState(() => _error = false);
                          _controller.loadRequest(Uri.parse(widget.url));
                        },
                        child: const Text('Reintentar'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              )
            : SafeArea(top: false, child: WebViewWidget(controller: _controller)),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### ARCHIVO: test\widget_test.dart
```python
// This is a basic Flutter widget test.
//
// To perform an interaction with a widget in your test, use the WidgetTester
// utility in the flutter_test package. For example, you can send tap and scroll
// gestures. You can also use WidgetTester to find child widgets in the widget
// tree, read text, and verify that the values of widget properties are correct.

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import 'package:gmb/main.dart';

void main() {
  testWidgets('Counter increments smoke test', (WidgetTester tester) async {
    // Build our app and trigger a frame.
    await tester.pumpWidget(const GijacApp());

    // Verify that our counter starts at 0.
    expect(find.text('0'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('1'), findsNothing);

    // Tap the '+' icon and trigger a frame.
    await tester.tap(find.byIcon(Icons.add));
    await tester.pump();

    // Verify that our counter has incremented.
    expect(find.text('0'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('1'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}

```
